# Translation review list

Generated by `node scripts/i18n-review.mjs` from `locales/review/<locale>.json`.
Every entry marked **REVIEW** needs a native speaker to check it; **clinical** entries need a
native-speaking clinician (physiotherapist or physician), **legal** entries need whoever signs
off consent and privacy wording. Edit the translation in `locales/<locale>/<namespace>.json`
(the key's first segment is the namespace file), then delete the entry from
`locales/review/<locale>.json` and re-run this script.

Decisions to confirm across all languages:
- Emergency number in `assessment.flags.limb.body`: de and es name 112; the others keep "your local emergency number".
- Cited press headlines (`stories.press.*.headline`) are translated; the German *Der Standard* headline is kept as published everywhere. Consider keeping the peer-reviewed paper title (`stories.press.jdr.headline`) in its original English.
- Formal address throughout (Sie / vous / u / usted / pt-PT third person / u).


## Português (pt-PT) — 603 entries

| | Key | Reason | English | Translation | Translator's note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REVIEW | `about.story.p1` | clinical | In 2019 our head of product woke up unable to close his left eye. He was diagnosed with Bell's palsy the same day, given steroids, and sent home with a photocopied sheet of nine facial exercises and no follow-up for four months. | Em 2019, o nosso responsável de produto acordou sem conseguir fechar o olho esquerdo. Foi-lhe diagnosticada paralisia de Bell no mesmo dia, recebeu corticoides e foi mandado para casa com uma fotocópia de nove exercícios faciais e sem qualquer acompanhamento durante quatro meses. | Diagnosis and steroids ('corticoides'). |
| REVIEW | `about.story.p2` | clinical | He did the exercises. Hard, twice a day, at maximum effort, for eleven weeks — which is close to the worst thing he could have done, and nobody told him otherwise until he found a specialist physiotherapist privately in month four. By then the synkinesis was established and it took another six months of coordination work to reduce it. | Fez os exercícios. Com força, duas vezes por dia, no esforço máximo, durante onze semanas — o que é quase a pior coisa que poderia ter feito, e ninguém lhe disse o contrário até encontrar, no quarto mês, um fisioterapeuta especializado no privado. Nessa altura, a sincinesia já estava instalada, e foram precisos mais seis meses de trabalho de coordenação para a reduzir. | Over-exercising leading to established synkinesis ('sincinesia já estava instalada'). |
| REVIEW | `about.story.p3` | clinical | The information that would have changed his recovery existed. It was in physiotherapy training, in specialist clinics, in journals. It was not in his hands, in his language, on the day he needed it. | A informação que teria mudado a sua recuperação existia. Estava na formação em fisioterapia, nas consultas especializadas, nas revistas científicas. Não estava nas mãos dele, na língua dele, no dia em que precisava dela. |  |
| REVIEW | `about.whatWeAreNot.body` | clinical | We are not a diagnostic service, we are not a replacement for medical care, and we are not a wellness product. We do not treat facial paralysis as a mindset problem. We will not tell you that your recovery depends on your attitude. | Não somos um serviço de diagnóstico, não substituímos os cuidados médicos e não somos um produto de bem-estar. Não tratamos a paralisia facial como uma questão de atitude mental. Não lhe vamos dizer que a sua recuperação depende da sua atitude. | Not a diagnostic service / not a replacement for medical care. |
| REVIEW | `about.whatWeAreNot.title` | clinical | What we are not | O que não somos |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.bilateral.body` | clinical | Facial weakness on both sides is uncommon and is more often linked to a systemic cause — Lyme disease, Guillain-Barré syndrome, sarcoidosis, among others. This should be investigated before you begin a rehabilitation programme. | A fraqueza facial dos dois lados é rara e está mais frequentemente associada a uma causa sistémica — doença de Lyme, síndrome de Guillain-Barré ou sarcoidose, entre outras. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.bilateral.title` | clinical | Both-sided weakness needs specialist assessment | A fraqueza dos dois lados precisa de avaliação especializada |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.estim.body` | clinical | We do not include electrical stimulation. The evidence is inconsistent and there is a theoretical concern that stimulation during reinnervation may worsen synkinesis. A evidência é inconsistente e existe a preocupação teórica de que a estimulação durante a reinervação possa agravar a sincinesia. Se um clínico lha receitou especificamente a si, siga o conselho dele — mas fale disso na sua primeira consulta. | E-stim during reinnervation ('reinervação') may worsen synkinesis. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.estim.title` | clinical | About the electrical stimulation | Sobre a estimulação elétrica |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.eye.body` | clinical | Eye pain, persistent redness, or changed vision with facial paralysis can mean the cornea is being damaged by exposure. This is the one complication that can cause lasting harm and it is treatable. Please get it looked at today — an optometrist, GP, or eye casualty department. | Dor no olho, vermelhidão persistente ou alterações da visão com paralisia facial podem significar que a córnea está a ser lesada por exposição. É a única complicação que pode causar danos permanentes, e tem tratamento. Peça que o vejam hoje — um optometrista, o seu médico de família ou uma urgência de oftalmologia. | Corneal exposure damage; 'optometrista, médico de família ou urgência de oftalmologia' for 'optometrist, GP, or eye casualty'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.eye.title` | clinical | See a doctor about your eye today | Consulte hoje um médico por causa do olho | See a doctor about the eye today. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.gum.body` | clinical | Chewing gum as facial exercise trains the jaw muscles rather than the facial muscles, and it reliably increases tightness and synkinesis. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.gum.title` | clinical | Please stop the chewing gum | Pare a pastilha elástica, por favor |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.late.body` | clinical | Your onset was {days, number} days ago, beyond the 5–90 day window our programme is designed around. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.limb.body` | clinical | Facial weakness together with weakness or numbness in an arm or leg can be a stroke. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.noEyeCare.body` | clinical | Your eye does not close fully and you are not using anything to protect it. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.progressive.body` | clinical | Bell's palsy comes on over hours to a few days and then stops getting worse. Diga especificamente ao seu médico que continua a piorar. | Progressive weakness needs imaging ('exames de imagem'). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.progressive.title` | clinical | Gradual, ongoing worsening needs investigation | Um agravamento gradual e contínuo tem de ser investigado |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.rash.body` | clinical | A rash or blisters near the ear alongside facial paralysis suggests Ramsay Hunt syndrome, which is treated with antivirals and has a different course from Bell's palsy. | REVIEW | `assessment.flags.tooEarly.body` | clinical | You are {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} from onset. Corticosteroids started within the first 72 hours measurably improve outcomes in Bell's palsy, and that window matters more right now than starting exercises. Nothing you do with the muscle in the first week changes the nerve.  Come back from day 5. We will hold your answers. | Passaram {days, plural, one {# dia} other {# dias}} desde o início. Os corticoides iniciados nas primeiras 72 horas melhoram de forma mensurável os resultados na paralisia de Bell, e essa janela importa mais neste momento do que começar os exercícios.
