| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingHint` | clinical | You will need elastic therapeutic tape. Any brand. Patch-test it on your forearm first. | Vai precisar de fita terapêutica elástica. De qualquer marca. Teste-a primeiro no antebraço. | Elastic therapeutic tape; patch-test on forearm. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingSummary` | clinical | {name} — {minutes} minutes. | {name} — {minutes} minutos. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingTitle` | clinical | Taping | Fita adesiva |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.trialTitle` | clinical | Your free 7 days start now | Os seus 7 dias grátis começam agora |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.clinicalNote` | clinical | <b>These questions were written by our clinical team.</b> The placement they produce follows the same reasoning a physiotherapist uses in a first appointment: time since onset, then what actually moves, then whether movements are linked. | <b>Estas perguntas foram escritas pela nossa equipa clínica.</b> A atribuição que resulta delas segue o mesmo raciocínio que um fisioterapeuta usa numa primeira consulta: tempo desde o início, depois o que realmente se mexe e depois se há movimentos ligados. | Placement reasoning mirrors physiotherapist first appointment. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.heading` | clinical | Sixteen questions, about eight minutes | Dezasseis perguntas, cerca de oito minutos |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.lead` | clinical | Your answers decide where your programme starts. There is no right answer and nothing to pass — an optimistic answer just gets you a programme that does not fit. | As suas respostas decidem onde começa o seu programa. Não há respostas certas nem nada para passar — uma resposta otimista só lhe dá um programa que não se ajusta a si. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.learnMore` | clinical | Read about the stages first | Ler primeiro sobre as fases |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.mirrorBody` | clinical | Three questions ask you to try a movement and watch what happens. A bathroom mirror in even light is ideal. | Três perguntas pedem-lhe que experimente um movimento e observe o que acontece. O ideal é um espelho de casa de banho com luz uniforme. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.mirrorTitle` | clinical | Have a mirror nearby | Tenha um espelho por perto |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.safetyBody` | clinical | Some answers mean you need a doctor rather than an app. If that comes up we stop and say so, plainly. | Algumas respostas significam que precisa de um médico e não de uma aplicação. Se isso acontecer, paramos e dizemo-lo, com clareza. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.safetyTitle` | clinical | We will tell you if we are wrong for you | Dizemos-lhe se não formos adequados para si |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.start` | clinical | Start the assessment | Iniciar a avaliação |  |
| REVIEW | `pricing.extras.extension.body` | clinical | Extend month to month at {price}, or stop. Most people in Stage 4 or 5 extend for two or three months and then move to a short maintenance routine on their own. | Prolongue mês a mês por {price}, ou pare. A maioria das pessoas na Fase 4 ou 5 prolonga dois ou três meses e depois passa para uma rotina curta de manutenção por conta própria. |  |
| REVIEW | `pricing.plans.guided.forWho` | clinical | The one most people choose. Two calls is usually enough to catch the mistakes that matter — taping tension, effort level, and synkinesis appearing. | O que a maioria das pessoas escolhe. Duas chamadas costumam chegar para apanhar os erros que importam — tensão da fita, nível de esforço e aparecimento de sincinesia. | Mistakes: tape tension, effort level, synkinesis appearing. |
| REVIEW | `pricing.plans.supported.forWho` | clinical | For more complex cases: significant synkinesis, post-surgical recovery, slow early progress, or anyone who simply wants regular contact with a person. | Para casos mais complexos: sincinesia significativa, recuperação após cirurgia, progresso inicial lento, ou quem simplesmente quer contacto regular com uma pessoa. | Complex cases: significant synkinesis, post-surgical recovery. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a1` | clinical | Forced or maximum-effort movements | Movimentos forçados ou de esforço máximo | Avoid forced/maximum-effort movements. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a2` | clinical | Electrical stimulation | Estimulação elétrica | Avoid electrical stimulation. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a3` | clinical | Chewing gum as "exercise" | Pastilha elástica como "exercício" | Chewing gum ('pastilha elástica'). |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a4` | clinical | Pushing through pain | Continuar apesar da dor | 'Pushing through pain' = 'Continuar apesar da dor'. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.body` | clinical | Nothing you do with the muscle will speed the nerve up in this stage, and pushing hard can make things harder later. So the programme is deliberately quiet: protect the eye, keep the tissue supple, support the resting position of the face, and let the inflammation settle. | Nada do que faça com o músculo vai acelerar o nervo nesta fase, e forçar pode dificultar as coisas mais tarde. Por isso, o programa é deliberadamente calmo: proteger o olho, manter os tecidos flexíveis, apoiar a posição de repouso do rosto e deixar a inflamação acalmar. | Stage 1 protection guidance; forcing may make things harder later. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f1` | clinical | Eye protection and lubrication | Proteção e lubrificação do olho | Eye protection and lubrication. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f2` | clinical | Passive support, no forced movement | Apoio passivo, sem movimentos forçados |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f3` | clinical | Gentle massage for circulation | Massagem suave para a circulação |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f4` | clinical | Sleep and rest | Sono e repouso |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.short` | clinical | The nerve is inflamed. The work is protection, not effort. | O nervo está inflamado. O trabalho é proteger, não esforçar. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.tapeMode` | clinical | Resting support — lifts the mid-face against gravity. No pull into movement. | Apoio em repouso — levanta a zona média do rosto contra a gravidade. Sem puxar para o movimento. | Resting support tape mode. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.window` | clinical | Usually days 1–14 from onset | Normalmente entre os dias 1 e 14 após o início |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a1` | clinical | Effortful straining, which recruits the wrong muscles | Esforço excessivo, que recruta os músculos errados | Effortful straining recruits wrong muscles. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a2` | clinical | Long sets — fatigue makes the pattern worse | Séries longas — o cansaço piora o padrão |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a3` | clinical | Comparing the two sides mid-exercise | Comparar os dois lados a meio do exercício |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.body` | clinical | A flicker is enough to work with. The goal now is to find the smallest movement you can actually produce and repeat it accurately, rather than to produce a big movement badly. Small and correct beats large and recruited. | Um esboço de movimento chega para trabalhar. O objetivo agora é encontrar o movimento mais pequeno que consegue realmente fazer e repeti-lo com precisão, em vez de fazer mal um movimento grande. Pequeno e correto é melhor do que grande e com recrutamento de outros músculos. | 'Flicker' of movement rendered 'esboço de movimento'; 'recruited' rendered 'com recrutamento de outros músculos'. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f1` | clinical | Finding minimum voluntary movement | Encontrar o movimento voluntário mínimo |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f2` | clinical | Low-effort, high-accuracy repetitions | Repetições com pouco esforço e muita precisão |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f4` | clinical | Keeping the unaffected side quiet | Manter o lado não afetado quieto |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.name` | clinical | Activate | Ativar |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.short` | clinical | The first signals are arriving. We help the brain find them. | Os primeiros sinais estão a chegar. Ajudamos o cérebro a encontrá-los. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.tapeMode` | clinical | Assist — tape helps the movement you are trying to make, so less effort is needed. | Assistência — a fita ajuda o movimento que está a tentar fazer, para que precise de menos esforço. | Assist tape mode. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.window` | clinical | Usually weeks 2–6, once any flicker of movement appears | Normalmente entre as semanas 2 e 6, assim que surge algum esboço de movimento |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a1` | clinical | Fast repetitions | Repetições rápidas |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a2` | clinical | Practising the linked pattern you are trying to unlearn | Praticar o padrão ligado que está a tentar desaprender |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a3` | clinical | Working past the point of fatigue | Continuar depois de chegar ao cansaço |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.body` | clinical | At this stage most people can produce movement but not isolate it — the eye narrows when you smile, or the corner of the mouth pulls when you close the eye. Coordination work teaches separation. This is slower and less satisfying than strength work, and it matters more. | Nesta fase, a maioria das pessoas consegue produzir movimento, mas não isolá-lo — o olho fecha-se um pouco quando sorri, ou o canto da boca puxa quando fecha o olho. O trabalho de coordenação ensina a separar. É mais lento e menos gratificante do que o trabalho de força, e é mais importante. | Synkinesis description (eye narrows on smiling). |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f1` | clinical | Separating eye movement from mouth movement | Separar o movimento do olho do movimento da boca |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f2` | clinical | Slow, controlled range through the middle of movement | Amplitude lenta e controlada a meio do movimento |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f3` | clinical | Holding a position rather than snapping to it | Manter uma posição em vez de a atingir de repente |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f4` | clinical | Relaxation between repetitions | Relaxar entre repetições |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.name` | clinical | Coordination | Coordenação |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.short` | clinical | Movement exists. Now it needs to go where you send it. | O movimento existe. Agora tem de ir para onde o manda. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.tapeMode` | clinical | Guide — tape gives a directional cue and discourages the linked movement. | Orientação — a fita dá uma indicação de direção e desencoraja o movimento ligado. | Guide tape mode. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.window` | clinical | Usually weeks 6–16 | Normalmente entre as semanas 6 e 16 |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.avoid.a1` | clinical | Maximum-effort holds | Manter posições com esforço máximo |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.avoid.a2` | clinical | Ignoring tightness because movement is present | Ignorar a rigidez porque já há movimento |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.body` | clinical | Refinement is about the movements you use without thinking: speaking, eating, expressions in conversation. The exercises get closer to real life and further from the mirror, and tension management becomes as important as activation. | Refinar tem a ver com os movimentos que faz sem pensar: falar, comer, as expressões numa conversa. Os exercícios aproximam-se da vida real e afastam-se do espelho, e gerir a tensão passa a ser tão importante como ativar. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f1` | clinical | Expression during speech | Expressão ao falar |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f2` | clinical | Symmetry at conversational effort, not maximum effort | Simetria com o esforço de uma conversa, não com esforço máximo |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f4` | clinical | Endurance across the day | Resistência ao longo do dia |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.name` | clinical | Refine | Refinar |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.short` | clinical | The face works. We are after control at low effort. | O rosto funciona. Procuramos controlo com pouco esforço. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.tapeMode` | clinical | Occasional — used for specific tasks or tiredness, not daily. | Ocasional — usada para tarefas específicas ou em caso de cansaço, não todos os dias. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.window` | clinical | Usually months 4–9 | Normalmente entre os meses 4 e 9 |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.avoid.a1` | clinical | Stopping entirely and losing range quietly | Parar completamente e perder amplitude sem dar por isso |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.body` | clinical | Recovery flattens out but does not usually reverse. A shorter maintenance routine protects range and keeps tightness from creeping back, with periodic scans so any drift is caught early rather than discovered late. | A recuperação estabiliza, mas normalmente não regride. Uma rotina de manutenção mais curta protege a amplitude e impede que a rigidez volte aos poucos, com análises periódicas para que qualquer desvio seja detetado cedo e não descoberto tarde. | Recovery plateaus but does not usually reverse. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f1` | clinical | Short daily maintenance routine | Rotina diária de manutenção curta |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f2` | clinical | Stretching and release work | Alongamentos e trabalho de relaxamento |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f3` | clinical | Periodic scans to confirm stability | Análises periódicas para confirmar a estabilidade |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f4` | clinical | Returning to full activity | Regresso à atividade plena |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.name` | clinical | Maintain | Manter |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.short` | clinical | Holding what you have built, and using it. | Conservar o que construiu, e usá-lo. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.tapeMode` | clinical | Not routine. Available for tiring days or specific events. | Não é de rotina. Disponível para dias cansativos ou ocasiões específicas. |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.window` | clinical | From around month 9 | A partir de cerca do mês 9 |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.taping.directionalGuide` | clinical | Directional guide | Orientação direcional |  |
| REVIEW | `programme.taping.restingSupport` | clinical | Resting support | Apoio em repouso |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.quote` | clinical | Stage-matching is the part that matters clinically. Handing every patient the same exercise sheet regardless of whether they have synkinesis is common practice and it is not good practice. This does better than that. | A adequação à fase é o que importa clinicamente. Dar a todos os doentes a mesma folha de exercícios, tenham ou não sincinesia, é prática comum e não é boa prática. Isto faz melhor do que isso. | Clinical claim about stage-matching. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.role` | clinical | Neurorehabilitation, university hospital | Neurorreabilitação, hospital universitário |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.where` | clinical | Lille | Lille |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.rahn.quote` | clinical | What is unusual here is the restraint. The programme does not promise recovery, does not grade patients against each other, and is explicit about where the evidence is thin. I refer patients to it and I read the progress reports they send me. | O que aqui é invulgar é a contenção. O programa não promete recuperação, não classifica os doentes uns em relação aos outros e é explícito sobre onde a evidência é escassa. Encaminho doentes para cá e leio os relatórios de progresso que me enviam. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.rahn.where` | clinical | Vienna | Viena |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.solberg.quote` | clinical | I use the scan timeline in my own clinic sessions. Being able to show a patient objective change during a nine-week plateau does something for them that my reassurance alone does not. | Uso a cronologia das análises nas minhas próprias sessões clínicas. Poder mostrar a um doente uma mudança objetiva durante nove semanas de estagnação faz-lhe algo que as minhas palavras de tranquilização, sozinhas, não fazem. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.solberg.where` | clinical | Oslo | Oslo |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.body` | clinical | Marta woke on a Tuesday unable to close her right eye. Steroids started within 48 hours. She began Palsya at week two, in Stage 1, and spent eleven days doing almost nothing but eye protection and light massage — which she describes as the hardest part of the whole programme. The first flicker in her cheek came at day 19. Synkinesis appeared around week seven, which she found more distressing than the original paralysis. Coordination work through Stages 3 and 4 reduced it substantially but not completely. Five months in, she has mild eye narrowing when she smiles broadly and full function otherwise. | A Marta acordou numa terça-feira sem conseguir fechar o olho direito. Começou os corticoides em menos de 48 horas. Iniciou a Palsya na segunda semana, na Fase 1, e passou onze dias a fazer quase só proteção do olho e massagem suave — o que descreve como a parte mais difícil de todo o programa. O primeiro esboço de movimento na bochecha surgiu ao 19.º dia. A sincinesia apareceu por volta da sétima semana, o que achou mais angustiante do que a paralisia inicial. O trabalho de coordenação nas Fases 3 e 4 reduziu-a bastante, mas não por completo. Ao fim de cinco meses, o olho fecha-se ligeiramente quando sorri muito, e de resto tem a função completa. | Clinical course narrative; synkinesis; steroids within 48 h. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.dx` | clinical | Bell's palsy | Paralisia de Bell |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.quote` | clinical | The stillness at the start felt like giving up. It was not. I understand that now and I did not then. | A quietude do início parecia desistir. Não era. Agora percebo isso, e na altura não percebia. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.summary` | clinical | From a complete right-sided palsy to eating and speaking normally, with mild synkinesis remaining. | De uma paralisia completa do lado direito a comer e falar normalmente, com uma ligeira sincinesia residual. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.where` | clinical | Warsaw | Varsóvia |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.body` | clinical | Sanne's paralysis followed surgery, which puts her in a different category from Bell's palsy: the recovery pattern was less predictable and the timeline longer. She used the six-consultation package throughout and stayed with the same clinician for nine months. Her mouth recovered substantially, her eye partially, and her forehead barely at all — an uneven pattern that she says was easier to accept once someone explained why it happens rather than treating it as a shortfall. She continues in Stage 5 with a short maintenance routine and a scan every six weeks. | A paralisia da Sanne surgiu depois de uma cirurgia, o que a coloca numa categoria diferente da paralisia de Bell: o padrão de recuperação foi menos previsível e o prazo mais longo. Usou o pacote de seis consultas do princípio ao fim e ficou com o mesmo clínico durante nove meses. A boca recuperou bastante, o olho em parte e a testa quase nada — um padrão desigual que, diz, foi mais fácil de aceitar quando alguém lhe explicou porque acontece em vez de o tratar como uma falha. Continua na Fase 5, com uma rotina curta de manutenção e uma análise de seis em seis semanas. | Post-surgical recovery pattern. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.dx` | clinical | Post-surgical | Pós-cirúrgica |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.quote` | clinical | I stopped measuring myself against the face I used to have. That was not a defeat. It was the thing that let me get on with it. | Deixei de me medir pelo rosto que tinha. Não foi uma derrota. Foi o que me permitiu seguir em frente. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.where` | clinical | Utrecht | Utrecht |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.body` | clinical | Tomás came to Palsya late, at week six, with no movement at all and having been told to wait and see. His first eight scans moved by less than four points, which is inside the measurement margin and therefore not really movement at all. He describes weeks seven through fifteen as the point where he stopped believing anything would change. His clinician kept him in Stage 2 through that entire period rather than advancing him, on the reasoning that advancing a programme ahead of the function is how compensations get established. Movement became clearly voluntary at week sixteen. By month seven his symmetry score had risen twenty-two points from baseline. | O Tomás chegou tarde à Palsya, na sexta semana, sem qualquer movimento e depois de lhe terem dito para esperar para ver. As suas primeiras oito análises variaram menos de quatro pontos, o que fica dentro da margem de medição e, portanto, não é bem movimento. Descreve as semanas sete a quinze como o momento em que deixou de acreditar que alguma coisa ia mudar. A sua clínica manteve-o na Fase 2 durante todo esse período em vez de o fazer avançar, com o argumento de que é ao adiantar o programa em relação à função que as compensações se instalam. O movimento tornou-se claramente voluntário na décima sexta semana. Ao sétimo mês, a sua pontuação de simetria tinha subido vinte e dois pontos em relação à linha de base. | Plateau, measurement margin, compensations ('compensações se instalam'). |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.dx` | clinical | Bell's palsy | Paralisia de Bell |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.quote` | clinical | Nine weeks of flat lines. My physio would not move me on and she was right, but I resented her for about a month. | Nove semanas de linhas planas. A minha fisioterapeuta não me deixava avançar e tinha razão, mas fiquei ressentido com ela durante cerca de um mês. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.summary` | clinical | A slow start, a nine-week plateau, and steady change after it. | Um início lento, nove semanas de estagnação e uma mudança constante a seguir. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.where` | clinical | Porto | Porto |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.aisling.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Cork · started at week 2 | Paralisia de Bell · Cork · começou na semana 2 |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.aisling.quote` | clinical | I could see the change in the photo timeline before I could see it in the mirror. That mattered more than I expected during the weeks where nothing seemed to be happening. | Consegui ver a mudança na cronologia de fotografias antes de a ver ao espelho. Isso contou mais do que esperava nas semanas em que parecia não estar a acontecer nada. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.dieter.ctx` | clinical | Ramsay Hunt syndrome · Leipzig · started at week 6 | Síndrome de Ramsay Hunt · Leipzig · começou na semana 6 |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.dieter.quote` | clinical | Ramsay Hunt, so a slower road than most. What I appreciated was that nobody pretended otherwise or promised me a timeline they could not know. | Ramsay Hunt, portanto um caminho mais lento do que a maioria. O que apreciei foi ninguém fingir o contrário nem me prometer um prazo que não podia saber. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.lena.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Malmö · started at week 1 | Paralisia de Bell · Malmö · começou na semana 1 |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.lena.quote` | clinical | The eye care instructions were more useful than anything I got at the hospital, where I was handed a leaflet and sent home. My cornea is fine and I think that is why. | As instruções de cuidados com o olho foram mais úteis do que tudo o que recebi no hospital, onde me deram um folheto e me mandaram para casa. A minha córnea está bem e acho que é por isso. | Cornea / eye care claim in testimonial. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.marek.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Kraków · started at week 3 | Paralisia de Bell · Cracóvia · começou na semana 3 |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.marek.quote` | clinical | The bit that helped most was being told to do less. I had been forcing the smile as hard as I could, twice a day, for five weeks. My physio on the second call spotted it in about a minute. | O que mais me ajudou foi dizerem-me para fazer menos. Andava a forçar o sorriso o mais que podia, duas vezes por dia, há cinco semanas. O meu fisioterapeuta, na segunda chamada, reparou nisso em cerca de um minuto. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.rosa.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Valencia · started at week 4 | Paralisia de Bell · Valência · começou na semana 4 |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.rosa.quote` | clinical | I am 71 and not good with apps. It is one screen with one button. My daughter set it up and I have used it on my own since the second day. | Tenho 71 anos e não me dou bem com aplicações. É um ecrã com um botão. A minha filha configurou-a e uso-a sozinha desde o segundo dia. |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.youssef.ctx` | clinical | Post-surgical facial nerve injury · Lyon | Lesão do nervo facial pós-cirúrgica · Lyon |  |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.youssef.quote` | clinical | Six months in, my face is not the face I had. But I can drink from a glass, I can eat in a restaurant, and I stopped avoiding photographs. That was the actual goal. | Ao fim de seis meses, o meu rosto não é o rosto que tinha. Mas consigo beber por um copo, consigo comer num restaurante e deixei de evitar fotografias. Era esse o verdadeiro objetivo. |  |
| REVIEW | `auth.signUp.footnote` | legal | No card required. You will not be charged before day 8. | Não é preciso cartão. Não será cobrado antes do dia 8. |  |
| REVIEW | `clinician.patient.notes.hint` | legal | Notes are part of the patient's record and can't be edited or deleted. Up to {max} characters. | As notas fazem parte do processo do doente e não podem ser editadas nem eliminadas. | REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.session.p2` | terminology | Every exercise is guided by a timer with spoken-style cues, a diagram, the repetition count for your stage, and the one thing most people get wrong about that particular movement. Taping shows you where each strip goes and how much tension — which is the mistake almost everyone makes, always in the direction of too much. | Cada exercício é guiado por um temporizador com indicações em tom falado, um diagrama, o número de repetições para a sua fase e o erro mais comum nesse movimento em particular. A parte da fita adesiva mostra-lhe onde vai cada tira e com quanta tensão — o erro que quase toda a gente comete, sempre por excesso. | 'Taping' rendered throughout as 'fita adesiva' (kinesio-type tape). Consider whether PT physios prefer 'ligadura funcional' / 'kinesio taping'. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.why` | terminology | The mid-face lifts the nasolabial fold and supports the upper lip. It responds well to small isolated work and is easy to skip. | A zona média do rosto levanta o sulco nasogeniano e apoia o lábio superior. Responde bem a trabalho pequeno e isolado, e é fácil de esquecer. | 'Nasolabial fold' = 'sulco nasogeniano'. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.name` | terminology | Lip pucker and seal | Biquinho e fecho dos lábios | 'Lip pucker' = 'Biquinho'; 'ooh' sound = 'u'. |

It is often the first change patients notice in daily life. | O fecho dos lábios é o que lhe permite beber, comer e dizer sons explosivos. Costuma ser a primeira mudança que os doentes notam no dia a dia. | 'Plosive sounds' = 'sons explosivos' (or 'oclusivos'). |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.body` | terminology | Plosive and labial sounds need lip seal, so they double as an exercise you can do without a mirror. ## Nederlands (nl-NL) — 610 entries

| | Key | Reason | English | Translation | Translator's note |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| REVIEW | `about.story.p1` | clinical | In 2019 our head of product woke up unable to close his left eye. He was diagnosed with Bell's palsy the same day, given steroids, and sent home with a photocopied sheet of nine facial exercises and no follow-up for four months. | In 2019 werd ons hoofd product wakker en kon hij zijn linkeroog niet meer sluiten. Dezelfde dag kreeg hij de diagnose Bell's parese, corticosteroïden en een gekopieerd velletje met negen gezichtsoefeningen mee naar huis. Vier maanden lang volgde er geen controle. | Steroids rendered as corticosteroïden. |
| REVIEW | `about.story.p2` | clinical | He did the exercises. Hard, twice a day, at maximum effort, for eleven weeks — which is close to the worst thing he could have done, and nobody told him otherwise until he found a specialist physiotherapist privately in month four. By then the synkinesis was established and it took another six months of coordination work to reduce it. | Hij deed de oefeningen. Hard, twee keer per dag, met maximale kracht, elf weken lang — zo ongeveer het slechtste wat hij kon doen, en niemand vertelde hem anders tot hij in de vierde maand op eigen kosten een gespecialiseerde fysiotherapeut vond. Toen was de synkinesie al ontstaan, en het kostte nog zes maanden coördinatietraining om die te verminderen. | Synkinesis = synkinesie; describes harm of max-effort exercise. |
| REVIEW | `about.story.p3` | clinical | The information that would have changed his recovery existed. It was in physiotherapy training, in specialist clinics, in journals. It was not in his hands, in his language, on the day he needed it. | De informatie die zijn herstel had kunnen veranderen, bestond al. In de opleiding fysiotherapie, in gespecialiseerde klinieken, in vakbladen. Alleen niet in zijn handen, in zijn taal, op de dag dat hij die nodig had. |  |
| REVIEW | `about.whatWeAreNot.body` | clinical | We are not a diagnostic service, we are not a replacement for medical care, and we are not a wellness product. We do not treat facial paralysis as a mindset problem. We will not tell you that your recovery depends on your attitude. | We zijn geen diagnostische dienst, we vervangen geen medische zorg en we zijn geen wellnessproduct. We behandelen aangezichtsverlamming niet als een kwestie van mindset. We zullen u niet vertellen dat uw herstel afhangt van uw houding. |  |
| REVIEW | `about.whatWeAreNot.title` | clinical | What we are not | Wat we niet zijn |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.bilateral.body` | clinical | Facial weakness on both sides is uncommon and is more often linked to a systemic cause — Lyme disease, Guillain-Barré syndrome, sarcoidosis, among others. This should be investigated before you begin a rehabilitation programme. | Zwakte aan beide kanten van het gezicht is zeldzaam en hangt vaker samen met een oorzaak elders in het lichaam — onder andere de ziekte van Lyme, het syndroom van Guillain-Barré of sarcoïdose. Dit moet worden onderzocht voordat u aan een revalidatieprogramma begint. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.bilateral.title` | clinical | Both-sided weakness needs specialist assessment | Zwakte aan beide kanten vraagt om beoordeling door een specialist | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.estim.body` | clinical | We do not include electrical stimulation. The evidence is inconsistent and there is a theoretical concern that stimulation during reinnervation may worsen synkinesis. If a clinician prescribed it for you specifically, follow their advice — but please raise it on your first consultation. | Elektrostimulatie maakt geen deel uit van ons programma. Het bewijs is wisselend, en er is een theoretisch risico dat stimulatie tijdens het herstel van de zenuw (reïnnervatie) synkinesie kan verergeren. Heeft een behandelaar het specifiek aan u voorgeschreven, volg dan dat advies — maar bespreek het wel tijdens uw eerste consult. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.estim.title` | clinical | About the electrical stimulation | Over de elektrostimulatie | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.eye.body` | clinical | Eye pain, persistent redness, or changed vision with facial paralysis can mean the cornea is being damaged by exposure. This is the one complication that can cause lasting harm and it is treatable. Please get it looked at today — an optometrist, GP, or eye casualty department. | Oogpijn, aanhoudende roodheid of veranderd zicht bij aangezichtsverlamming kan betekenen dat het hoornvlies beschadigd raakt doordat het oog open blijft. Dit is de enige complicatie die blijvende schade kan veroorzaken, en ze is te behandelen. Laat het vandaag nog nakijken — door een optometrist, uw huisarts of de spoedeisende oogzorg. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.eye.title` | clinical | See a doctor about your eye today | Laat uw oog vandaag nog door een arts bekijken | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.gum.body` | clinical | Chewing gum as facial exercise trains the jaw muscles rather than the facial muscles, and it reliably increases tightness and synkinesis. It is one of the few things we would ask you to stop outright. | Kauwgom kauwen als gezichtsoefening traint de kauwspieren in plaats van de gezichtsspieren, en het verergert stijfheid en synkinesie vrijwel altijd. Het is een van de weinige dingen waarvan we u vragen er meteen mee te stoppen. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.gum.title` | clinical | Please stop the chewing gum | Stop alstublieft met kauwgom | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.late.body` | clinical | Your onset was {days, number} days ago, beyond the 5–90 day window our programme is designed around. Structured rehabilitation still helps at this point, particularly for synkinesis and tightness, but the starting point needs a person to set it. A clinician will review your assessment within one working day before your programme begins. | Uw klachten begonnen {days, number} dagen geleden, buiten het tijdvenster van 5–90 dagen waarvoor ons programma is ontworpen. Gestructureerde revalidatie helpt op dit moment nog steeds, vooral bij synkinesie en stijfheid, maar het startpunt moet door een mens worden bepaald. Een behandelaar beoordeelt uw intake binnen één werkdag, voordat uw programma begint. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.late.title` | clinical | Outside our usual window — a clinician will review | Buiten ons gebruikelijke tijdvenster — een behandelaar kijkt mee | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.limb.body` | clinical | Facial weakness together with weakness or numbness in an arm or leg can be a stroke. This needs emergency assessment, not a rehabilitation programme. Please stop here and call your local emergency number. | Zwakte in het gezicht samen met krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid in een arm of been kan een beroerte zijn. Dit vraagt om spoedeisende beoordeling, niet om een revalidatieprogramma. Stop hier en bel het alarmnummer in uw land. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.limb.title` | clinical | Call emergency services now | Bel nu het alarmnummer | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.noEyeCare.body` | clinical | Your eye does not close fully and you are not using anything to protect it. Preservative-free lubricating drops are available without prescription and should start today, with ointment at night. This matters more than any exercise in your programme. | Uw oog sluit niet helemaal en u gebruikt niets om het te beschermen. Bevochtigende oogdruppels zonder conserveermiddel zijn zonder recept verkrijgbaar. Begin daar vandaag mee, met oogzalf voor de nacht. Dit is belangrijker dan welke oefening in uw programma ook. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.noEyeCare.title` | clinical | Start eye protection today | Begin vandaag met het beschermen van uw oog | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.progressive.body` | clinical | Bell's palsy comes on over hours to a few days and then stops getting worse. Weakness that is still progressing over weeks can point to a different cause that needs imaging. Please tell your doctor specifically that it is still worsening. | Bell's parese ontstaat in enkele uren tot een paar dagen en wordt daarna niet verder erger. Zwakte die na weken nog toeneemt, kan wijzen op een andere oorzaak waarvoor beeldvorming nodig is. Vertel uw arts uitdrukkelijk dat het nog steeds erger wordt. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.progressive.title` | clinical | Gradual, ongoing worsening needs investigation | Geleidelijke, aanhoudende verslechtering moet worden onderzocht | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.rash.body` | clinical | A rash or blisters near the ear alongside facial paralysis suggests Ramsay Hunt syndrome, which is treated with antivirals and has a different course from Bell's palsy. Getting the right diagnosis changes the treatment, so please see a doctor. | Huiduitslag of blaasjes bij het oor samen met aangezichtsverlamming wijzen op het syndroom van Ramsay Hunt. Dat wordt behandeld met antivirale middelen en verloopt anders dan Bell's parese. De juiste diagnose verandert de behandeling, dus ga naar een arts. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.rash.title` | clinical | The rash should be assessed | De huiduitslag moet worden beoordeeld | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.tooEarly.body` | clinical | You are {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} from onset. Corticosteroids started within the first 72 hours measurably improve outcomes in Bell's palsy, and that window matters more right now than starting exercises. Nothing you do with the muscle in the first week changes the nerve.  Come back from day 5. We will hold your answers. | Het is {days, plural, one {# dag} other {# dagen}} geleden dat de klachten begonnen. Corticosteroïden die binnen de eerste 72 uur worden gestart, verbeteren de uitkomst bij Bell's parese aantoonbaar, en dat tijdvenster is nu belangrijker dan beginnen met oefeningen. Niets wat u in de eerste week met de spieren doet, verandert iets aan de zenuw.  Kom vanaf dag 5 terug. We bewaren uw antwoorden. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.tooEarly.title` | clinical | It is very early — medical treatment comes first | Het is nog heel vroeg — medische behandeling gaat voor | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.undiagnosed.body` | clinical | Palsya is a rehabilitation programme, not a diagnostic service. Facial weakness has several causes, some of them urgent, and some are treated with medication that works best in the first few days. Please see a doctor first. | Palsya is een revalidatieprogramma, geen diagnostische dienst. Zwakte in het gezicht heeft verschillende oorzaken. Sommige zijn urgent, en sommige worden behandeld met medicijnen die in de eerste dagen het best werken. Ga eerst naar een arts. | Safety flag body. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.undiagnosed.title` | clinical | You need a diagnosis before you start | U hebt eerst een diagnose nodig | Safety flag title. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.flicker.d` | clinical | Something moves, but barely — I may be the only one who can see it. | Er beweegt iets, maar nauwelijks — misschien zie alleen ik het. | Movement grading description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.flicker.t` | clinical | A flicker | Een trilling | Movement grading option; flicker = 'trilling'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.near.d` | clinical | Close to the other side, or the same. | Bijna gelijk aan de andere kant, of hetzelfde. | Movement grading description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.near.t` | clinical | Nearly normal | Bijna normaal | Movement grading option; flicker = 'trilling'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.none.d` | clinical | Nothing happens when I try. | Er gebeurt niets als ik het probeer. | Movement grading description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.none.t` | clinical | No movement at all | Helemaal geen beweging | Movement grading option; flicker = 'trilling'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.partial.d` | clinical | It moves, clearly less than the other side. | Het beweegt, duidelijk minder dan de andere kant. | Movement grading description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.optionSets.move.partial.t` | clinical | Partial movement | Gedeeltelijke beweging | Movement grading option; flicker = 'trilling'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.help` | clinical | If you are not sure of the exact name, choose the closest option. | Weet u de precieze naam niet, kies dan de optie die er het dichtst bij komt. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.other.d` | clinical | A different diagnosed cause. | Een andere vastgestelde oorzaak. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.other.t` | clinical | Something else | Iets anders |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.surgical.t` | clinical | After surgery | Na een operatie |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.trauma.t` | clinical | After an injury | Na letsel |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.q` | clinical | What diagnosis have you been given? | Welke diagnose hebt u gekregen? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeCare.q` | clinical | What are you currently using to protect the eye? | Wat gebruikt u op dit moment om het oog te beschermen? | Eye protection question. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.help` | clinical | Look in a mirror and close both eyes gently, without forcing. | Kijk in een spiegel en sluit beide ogen rustig, zonder te forceren. | Instruction not to force closure. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.options.almost.t` | clinical | Almost — a small gap remains | Bijna — er blijft een kleine spleet over | Lid closure option; residual gap = 'spleet' (lagophthalmos). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.options.full.t` | clinical | Yes, fully | Ja, helemaal | Lid closure option; residual gap = 'spleet' (lagophthalmos). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.options.none.t` | clinical | No, the lid does not move | Nee, het ooglid beweegt niet | Lid closure option; residual gap = 'spleet' (lagophthalmos). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.options.partial.t` | clinical | Partly — the lid moves but a clear gap remains | Gedeeltelijk — het ooglid beweegt, maar er blijft een duidelijke spleet | Lid closure option; residual gap = 'spleet' (lagophthalmos). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeClosure.q` | clinical | Can you close the affected eye? | Kunt u het aangedane oog sluiten? | Eye closure assessment. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.help` | clinical | Select everything that applies. This is the most important question on this page. | Kies alles wat van toepassing is. Dit is de belangrijkste vraag op deze pagina. | Most important question on the page. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.dryness.t` | clinical | Dryness or grittiness | Droogte of een zanderig gevoel | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.none.t` | clinical | None of these | Geen van deze | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.pain.t` | clinical | Pain in the eye | Pijn in het oog | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.redness.t` | clinical | Redness that does not settle | Roodheid die niet overgaat | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.vision.t` | clinical | Blurred or changed vision | Wazig of veranderd zicht | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.watering.t` | clinical | Excessive watering | Overmatig tranen | Eye symptom wording ('zanderig gevoel' for grittiness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.q` | clinical | Do you have any of these eye symptoms right now? | Hebt u op dit moment een van deze oogklachten? | Eye red-flag symptoms. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.help` | clinical | This shapes which parts of the daily routine we put in front of you first. | Dit bepaalt welke onderdelen van de dagelijkse routine we u als eerste aanbieden. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.drinking.t` | clinical | Drinking without spilling | Drinken zonder te morsen |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.eating.t` | clinical | Eating — food collects in the cheek | Eten — er blijft voedsel in de wang zitten |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.eye.t` | clinical | Managing the eye | De zorg voor mijn oog |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.none.t` | clinical | None of these are difficult | Geen van deze is moeilijk |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.sleep.t` | clinical | Sleeping | Slapen |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.social.t` | clinical | Being around other people | Onder de mensen zijn |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.options.speaking.t` | clinical | Being understood when I speak | Verstaanbaar praten |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.impact.q` | clinical | Which of these are difficult right now? | Welke van deze dingen zijn op dit moment moeilijk? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.medical.options.no.t` | clinical | Not yet | Nog niet | Steroids = corticosteroïden (patients may know it as prednison). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.medical.options.noSteroids.t` | clinical | Yes, but no steroids were prescribed | Ja, maar ik heb geen corticosteroïden gekregen | Steroids = corticosteroïden (patients may know it as prednison). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.medical.options.steroids.t` | clinical | Yes, and I was prescribed steroids | Ja, en ik heb corticosteroïden voorgeschreven gekregen | Steroids = corticosteroïden (patients may know it as prednison). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.medical.options.waiting.t` | clinical | I have an appointment booked | Ik heb een afspraak gemaakt | Steroids = corticosteroïden (patients may know it as prednison). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.medical.q` | clinical | Have you been assessed by a doctor? | Bent u door een arts onderzocht? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.moveBrow.help` | clinical | Hold the unaffected eyebrow still with two fingers so it cannot do the work. | Houd de wenkbrauw aan de gezonde kant met twee vingers stil, zodat die het werk niet overneemt. | Movement self-test instruction. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.moveBrow.q` | clinical | Try to raise your eyebrows. What happens on the affected side? | Probeer uw wenkbrauwen op te trekken. Wat gebeurt er aan de aangedane kant? | Movement self-test instruction. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.moveEye.q` | clinical | Try to close the affected eye tightly. How much does it move? | Probeer het aangedane oog stevig dicht te knijpen. Hoeveel beweegt het? | Movement self-test instruction. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.moveMouth.q` | clinical | Try a small smile with your lips together. What happens on the affected side? | Probeer een kleine glimlach met de lippen op elkaar. Wat gebeurt er aan de aangedane kant? | Movement self-test instruction. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.onsetDate.help` | clinical | Your best estimate is fine. If it came on overnight, use the morning you noticed it. | Een zo goed mogelijke schatting is prima. Kwam het 's nachts op, kies dan de ochtend waarop u het merkte. | Onset date guidance. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.onsetDate.q` | clinical | When did the facial weakness start? | Wanneer begon de zwakte in uw gezicht? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.earPain.t` | clinical | Ear pain | Oorpijn | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.hearing.t` | clinical | Hearing loss or new sound sensitivity | Gehoorverlies of nieuwe overgevoeligheid voor geluid | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.limb.t` | clinical | Weakness or numbness in an arm or leg | Krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid in een arm of been | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.none.t` | clinical | None of these | Geen van deze | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.rash.t` | clinical | A rash or blisters near the ear, in the ear, or on the scalp | Huiduitslag of blaasjes bij het oor, in het oor of op de hoofdhuid | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.taste.t` | clinical | Altered taste | Veranderde smaak | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.tinnitus.t` | clinical | Ringing in the ear | Oorsuizen | Associated symptom / red flag (oorsuizen for tinnitus, krachtverlies of gevoelloosheid for limb weakness). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.q` | clinical | Do you have any of these? | Hebt u een van deze klachten? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.help` | clinical | Two shorter sessions works better than one long one, but one done consistently beats two abandoned. | Twee kortere sessies werken beter dan één lange, maar één sessie die u volhoudt is beter dan twee die u opgeeft. | Dosing advice. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.options.1.d` | clinical | Around 10–20 minutes. | Ongeveer 10–20 minuten. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.options.1.t` | clinical | One session | Eén sessie |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.options.2.d` | clinical | Around 8–10 minutes each. Recommended. | Elk ongeveer 8–10 minuten. Aanbevolen. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.options.2.t` | clinical | Two sessions | Twee sessies |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.perDay.q` | clinical | How many sessions a day suits your life? | Hoeveel sessies per dag passen in uw leven? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.help` | clinical | This helps us understand whether the pattern is typical. | Zo kunnen we inschatten of het patroon gebruikelijk is. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.days.d` | clinical | It got worse across two or three days, then stopped. | Het werd in twee à drie dagen erger en stopte toen. | Onset pattern description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.days.t` | clinical | Over a few days | In een paar dagen | Onset pattern option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.gradual.d` | clinical | Slowly worsening over weeks. | Langzaam erger over een periode van weken. | Onset pattern description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.gradual.t` | clinical | Gradually, and it is still getting worse | Geleidelijk, en het wordt nog steeds erger | Onset pattern option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.recurring.d` | clinical | A previous episode on the same or the other side. | Een eerdere keer aan dezelfde of de andere kant. | Onset pattern description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.recurring.t` | clinical | This has happened before | Dit is al eens eerder gebeurd | Onset pattern option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.sudden.d` | clinical | Over hours, or I woke up with it. | Binnen enkele uren, of ik werd ermee wakker. | Onset pattern description. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.options.sudden.t` | clinical | Suddenly | Plotseling | Onset pattern option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.progression.q` | clinical | How did the weakness come on? | Hoe ontstond de zwakte? | Onset pattern (red flag screening). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.side.options.both.t` | clinical | Both sides | Beide kanten | Bilateral = red flag. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.side.options.left.t` | clinical | Left side | Linkerkant |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.side.options.right.t` | clinical | Right side | Rechterkant |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.side.q` | clinical | Which side of your face is affected? | Welke kant van uw gezicht is aangedaan? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.help` | clinical | Or the reverse: when you close the eye, does the corner of the mouth pull? Either counts. | Of andersom: als u het oog sluit, trekt de mondhoek dan mee? Allebei telt. | Reverse synkinesis pattern. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.options.always.t` | clinical | Always — I cannot separate them | Altijd — ik kan ze niet los van elkaar bewegen | Synkinesis frequency option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.options.frequent.t` | clinical | Often, with most movements | Vaak, bij de meeste bewegingen | Synkinesis frequency option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.options.none.t` | clinical | No, they move separately | Nee, ze bewegen los van elkaar | Synkinesis frequency option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.options.occasional.t` | clinical | Occasionally, with bigger movements | Soms, bij grotere bewegingen | Synkinesis frequency option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.options.unsure.t` | clinical | I am not sure | Ik weet het niet zeker | Synkinesis frequency option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.synkinesis.q` | clinical | When you smile, does the affected eye narrow or close on its own? | Als u glimlacht, wordt het aangedane oog dan vanzelf kleiner of gaat het dicht? | Synkinesis screening question, lay phrasing. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.tightness.options.mild.t` | clinical | A little tight, mostly by the end of the day | Een beetje strak, vooral aan het eind van de dag | Tightness grading option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.tightness.options.moderate.t` | clinical | Noticeably tight most of the time | Merkbaar strak, het grootste deel van de tijd | Tightness grading option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.tightness.options.none.t` | clinical | No, it feels loose or normal | Nee, het voelt slap of normaal | Tightness grading option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.tightness.options.severe.t` | clinical | Very tight — it limits how much I can move | Heel strak — het beperkt hoeveel ik kan bewegen | Tightness grading option. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.treatment.q` | clinical | Are you doing any of these already? | Doet u al een van deze dingen? |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.clinicianOverride` | clinical | Your clinician updated your stage after reviewing your progress. | Uw behandelaar heeft uw fase aangepast na het beoordelen van uw voortgang. |  |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.eyeProtection` | clinical | Because the eyelid does not move, eye protection stays at the front of your daily routine regardless of stage. | Omdat het ooglid niet beweegt, blijft oogbescherming vooraan in uw dagelijkse routine staan, in welke fase u ook zit. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.maintenance` | clinical | More than nine months from onset with near-normal movement and little linked movement — the work now is maintaining range rather than building it. | Meer dan negen maanden na het begin, met bijna normale beweging en weinig meebewegingen — het werk is nu de bewegingsruimte behouden in plaats van opbouwen. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.minimalMovement` | clinical | Early voluntary movement is present but minimal, so the work is finding and repeating the smallest accurate movement. | Er is al wat bewuste beweging, maar minimaal. Het werk bestaat daarom uit het vinden en herhalen van de kleinste nauwkeurige beweging. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.nearNormal` | clinical | Movement is close to normal in most regions, so the work moves to control at everyday effort levels. | In de meeste gebieden is de beweging bijna normaal, dus het werk verschuift naar controle bij de inspanning van alledag. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.noMovement` | clinical | No voluntary movement in any region, so the programme starts with protection rather than activation. | Er is in geen enkel gebied bewuste beweging, dus het programma begint met bescherming in plaats van activatie. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.notIsolated` | clinical | Movement is present across most of the face but not yet isolated, which puts coordination at the centre of your programme. | Het grootste deel van het gezicht beweegt, maar nog niet los van elkaar. Daarom staat coördinatie centraal in uw programma. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.severeTightness` | clinical | Severe tightness limits range more than weakness does, so release and coordination work take priority. | Ernstige stijfheid beperkt de bewegingsruimte meer dan de zwakte, dus ontspannings- en coördinatieoefeningen gaan voor. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.slowRecovery` | clinical | Recovery after {cause, select, ramsay {Ramsay Hunt syndrome} surgical {surgery} other {injury}} is typically slower and less even than after Bell's palsy, so we will not read a slow start as a poor sign. | Herstel na {cause, select, ramsay {het syndroom van Ramsay Hunt} surgical {een operatie} other {een letsel}} verloopt meestal langzamer en minder gelijkmatig dan na Bell's parese. Een trage start zien we daarom niet als een slecht teken. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.synkinesisHeldBack` | clinical | Movement quality would suggest a later stage, but frequent synkinesis is best addressed with coordination work first. | Op basis van de kwaliteit van de beweging zou een latere fase passen, maar veel synkinesie pakt u het best eerst aan met coördinatietraining. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.synkinesisRaised` | clinical | You described frequent linked movement. Coordination work is the priority when synkinesis is established, even where movement is still limited. | U beschrijft vaak voorkomende meebewegingen. Als synkinesie is ontstaan, heeft coördinatietraining voorrang, ook als de beweging nog beperkt is. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.timeEarliest` | clinical | At {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} from onset, Stage {stage} is the earliest placement that makes clinical sense. | {days, plural, one {# dag} other {# dagen}} na het begin is fase {stage} de vroegste indeling die klinisch zinvol is. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.timeHoldBack` | clinical | Your answers alone would suggest Stage {suggested}, but at {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} from onset we hold back a stage. Advancing ahead of the nerve is how compensations get established. | Op basis van alleen uw antwoorden zou fase {suggested} passen, maar {days, plural, one {# dag} other {# dagen}} na het begin houden we een fase in. Vooruitlopen op de zenuw is precies hoe compensaties ontstaan. | Stage-placement reasoning shown to patient. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reviewWhy.lateOnset` | clinical | your onset is outside our usual window | uw klachten begonnen buiten ons gebruikelijke tijdvenster | Reason for clinician review (sentence fragment inserted after 'Dat komt doordat'). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reviewWhy.lowConfidence` | clinical | some of your answers do not sit together in the usual pattern | sommige van uw antwoorden passen niet in het gebruikelijke patroon | Reason for clinician review (sentence fragment inserted after 'Dat komt doordat'). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reviewWhy.otherDiagnosis` | clinical | your diagnosis is outside the ones we place automatically | uw diagnose valt buiten de diagnoses die we automatisch indelen | Reason for clinician review (sentence fragment inserted after 'Dat komt doordat'). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reviewWhy.surgeryOrInjury` | clinical | recovery after surgery or injury needs an individual starting point | herstel na een operatie of letsel vraagt om een individueel startpunt | Reason for clinician review (sentence fragment inserted after 'Dat komt doordat'). |
| REVIEW | `clinician.patient.notes.hint` | clinical | Notes are part of the patient's record and can't be edited or deleted. Up to {max} characters. | Notities maken deel uit van het dossier van de patiënt en kunnen niet worden bewerkt of verwijderd. Maximaal {max} tekens. | Record = "dossier van de patiënt"; notes immutable wording preserved. |
| REVIEW | `clinician.patient.stage.intro` | clinical | Set the patient's stage when your assessment differs from the programme's. The change is recorded in the stage history. | Stel de fase van de patiënt in als uw beoordeling afwijkt van die van het programma. De wijziging wordt vastgelegd in de fasegeschiedenis. |  |
| REVIEW | `clinician.patients.columns.onset` | clinical | Onset date | Begindatum | Onset date rendered as "Begindatum" (onset of paralysis); could be "Datum ontstaan". |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.library.logHint` | clinical | Function often changes before geometry does. What you notice is data too. | De functie verandert vaak eerder dan de vorm van het gezicht. Wat u opmerkt, telt ook als gegeven. | Function vs geometry claim. |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.goal` | clinical | Goal | Doel |  |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.markDone` | clinical | Mark taping done | Tapen gedaan |  |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.none` | clinical | No technique assigned for this stage | Geen techniek toegewezen voor deze fase |  |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.stripLine` | clinical | {from} → {to}. {note} | {from} → {to}. {note} |  |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.tension` | clinical | Tension | Spanning |  |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.title` | clinical | Taping | Tapen |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.daily-routine.blurb` | clinical | Eye care, eating, sleep, and fitting a programme into a life that already exists. | Oogzorg, eten, slapen, en een programma inpassen in een leven dat al bestaat. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.daily-routine.title` | clinical | Building a daily routine that holds | Een dagelijkse routine die u volhoudt |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.facial-exercises-guide.blurb` | clinical | Why small and accurate beats big and forceful, and the mistakes that make recovery harder. | Waarom klein en nauwkeurig beter is dan groot en krachtig, en welke fouten het herstel moeilijker maken. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.facial-exercises-guide.title` | clinical | Facial exercises: how to do them properly | Gezichtsoefeningen: hoe u ze goed doet |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.facial-taping-guide.blurb` | clinical | What the tape does, what it does not do, how to apply it safely, and when to take it off. | Wat de tape wel en niet doet, hoe u die veilig aanbrengt en wanneer u die weer verwijdert. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.facial-taping-guide.title` | clinical | Facial taping: a practical guide | Tapen van het gezicht: een praktische gids |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.faq.blurb` | clinical | Eligibility, timelines, cost, data, and the questions people ask before signing up. | Wie in aanmerking komt, tijdlijnen, kosten, gegevens en de vragen die mensen stellen voordat ze zich aanmelden. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.faq.title` | clinical | Frequently asked questions | Veelgestelde vragen |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.research-evidence.blurb` | clinical | What the literature supports, what is uncertain, and where our own numbers come from. | Wat de literatuur ondersteunt, wat onzeker is, en waar onze eigen cijfers vandaan komen. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.research-evidence.title` | clinical | Research and evidence | Onderzoek en bewijs |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.stages-of-recovery.blurb` | clinical | Five stages, what the body is doing in each, and why the programme changes shape as you move through them. | Vijf fases, wat het lichaam in elke fase doet, en waarom het programma verandert naarmate u verder komt. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.stages-of-recovery.title` | clinical | The stages of recovery | De fases van herstel |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.what-is-pfp.blurb` | clinical | What has happened to the nerve, why one side of the face stopped moving, and what that does and does not mean. | Wat er met de zenuw is gebeurd, waarom één kant van het gezicht niet meer beweegt, en wat dat wel en niet betekent. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.what-is-pfp.title` | clinical | What is peripheral facial paralysis? | Wat is perifere aangezichtsverlamming? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.diagnosis.a` | clinical | Yes. Palsya is a rehabilitation programme, not a diagnostic service. Facial weakness has causes that need medical investigation, some of them urgent. See a doctor first and come to us with a diagnosis. | Ja. Palsya is een revalidatieprogramma, geen diagnostische dienst. Zwakte in het gezicht kan oorzaken hebben die medisch onderzoek vragen, en sommige zijn urgent. Ga eerst naar een arts en kom met een diagnose bij ons. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.diagnosis.q` | clinical | Do I need a diagnosis first? | Heb ik eerst een diagnose nodig? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.install.a` | clinical | No. Palsya runs in your browser on a phone, tablet, or computer. You will need to allow camera access for scans, and a reasonably steady internet connection. | Nee. Palsya werkt in uw browser op een telefoon, tablet of computer. Voor de scans moet u toegang tot de camera toestaan, en u hebt een redelijk stabiele internetverbinding nodig. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.install.q` | clinical | Do I need to install anything? | Moet ik iets installeren? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.rightForMe.a` | clinical | Palsya is built for people with peripheral facial paralysis — Bell's palsy, Ramsay Hunt, and post-surgical or post-traumatic facial nerve injury — whose onset was between 5 and 90 days ago. If your onset was more recent than 5 days, you should be under medical assessment first; steroids given early make a measurable difference and that window matters more than starting rehabilitation. If it was longer than 90 days ago, a structured programme can still help, but you should speak to a clinician about the right starting point. Our assessment will tell you honestly if we are not the right fit. | Palsya is gemaakt voor mensen met een perifere aangezichtsverlamming — Bell's parese, het syndroom van Ramsay Hunt, en zenuwletsel van het aangezicht na een operatie of ongeval — bij wie de klachten 5 tot 90 dagen geleden begonnen. Begonnen de klachten minder dan 5 dagen geleden, dan hoort u eerst medisch te worden beoordeeld: corticosteroïden die vroeg worden gegeven, maken een meetbaar verschil, en dat tijdvenster is belangrijker dan beginnen met revalidatie. Is het langer dan 90 dagen geleden, dan kan een gestructureerd programma nog steeds helpen, maar bespreek het juiste startpunt met een behandelaar. Onze intake vertelt u eerlijk als we niet bij u passen. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.rightForMe.q` | clinical | Is Palsya right for me? | Is Palsya iets voor mij? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.technology.a` | clinical | The daily session is one screen with one button. Scans are guided step by step with plain instructions. If you get stuck, support answers in your language and can walk you through it on a call. | De dagelijkse sessie is één scherm met één knop. De scans worden stap voor stap begeleid met eenvoudige instructies. Komt u er niet uit, dan helpt support u in uw eigen taal en kan u er ook telefonisch doorheen loodsen. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.technology.q` | clinical | What if I am not confident with technology? | Wat als ik niet zo handig ben met technologie? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.title` | clinical | Getting started | Aan de slag |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.missDays.a` | clinical | Nothing happens. Your programme does not reset, nothing is lost, and we will not send you guilt. Open the app and do today's session. If a pattern of missed days appears, your clinician may ask about it — usually because the programme needs adjusting, not because you do. | Dan gebeurt er niets. Uw programma begint niet opnieuw, er gaat niets verloren en we gaan u geen schuldgevoel aanpraten. Open de app en doe de sessie van vandaag. Als u vaker dagen overslaat, kan uw behandelaar ernaar vragen — meestal omdat het programma moet worden aangepast, niet u. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.missDays.q` | clinical | What if I miss days? | Wat als ik dagen oversla? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.physio.a` | clinical | Yes, and many people do. Tell your therapist you are using Palsya and share your progress reports with them. If their guidance conflicts with ours, follow theirs — they can see you. | Ja, en veel mensen doen dat. Vertel uw therapeut dat u Palsya gebruikt en deel uw voortgangsrapporten. Als hun advies afwijkt van het onze, volg dan het hunne — zij kunnen u zien. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.physio.q` | clinical | Can I use this alongside in-person physiotherapy? | Kan ik dit combineren met fysiotherapie in de praktijk? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.recovery.a` | clinical | We will not answer that, because nobody honestly can. Most people with Bell's palsy recover well. A minority retain lasting asymmetry. We will not predict which group you are in, and you should be wary of anyone who does. | Die vraag beantwoorden we niet, omdat niemand dat eerlijk kan. De meeste mensen met Bell's parese herstellen goed. Een minderheid houdt blijvende asymmetrie. We voorspellen niet bij welke groep u hoort, en wees op uw hoede voor iedereen die dat wel doet. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.recovery.q` | clinical | Will I definitely recover? | Herstel ik zeker? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.timePerDay.a` | clinical | Between 10 and 20 minutes depending on your stage, usually split into two shorter sessions. Stage 1 is the shortest because it is mostly protection. Stage 3 is the longest because coordination work needs rest between repetitions. | Tussen de 10 en 20 minuten, afhankelijk van uw fase, meestal verdeeld over twee kortere sessies. Fase 1 is het kortst, omdat die vooral uit bescherming bestaat. Fase 3 is het langst, omdat coördinatietraining rust tussen de herhalingen nodig heeft. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.timePerDay.q` | clinical | How much time does it take each day? | Hoeveel tijd kost het per dag? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.title` | clinical | The programme | Het programma |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.howScan.a` | clinical | You position your face in an outline and follow four prompts — rest, brow raise, gentle smile, eye closure. Facial landmarks are detected and the geometry of your affected side is compared with the mirrored unaffected side. The result is a symmetry score out of 100 relative to your own baseline. | U plaatst uw gezicht in een omlijning en volgt vier aanwijzingen: in rust, wenkbrauwen optrekken, zachte glimlach, oog sluiten. Er worden herkenningspunten in uw gezicht gedetecteerd, en de vorm van uw aangedane kant wordt vergeleken met het spiegelbeeld van de gezonde kant. Het resultaat is een symmetriescore van 0 tot 100, ten opzichte van uw eigen nulmeting. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.howScan.q` | clinical | How does the scan work? | Hoe werkt de scan? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.photos.a` | clinical | Landmark detection runs on your device — the images themselves are not sent anywhere for processing. Your photos are stored only if you consent to it, and only so you can see your own timeline. You can delete any photo, or all of them, at any time. | De herkenningspunten worden op uw eigen apparaat gedetecteerd — de beelden zelf worden nergens heen gestuurd voor verwerking. Uw foto's worden alleen bewaard als u daar toestemming voor geeft, en alleen zodat u uw eigen tijdlijn kunt zien. U kunt elke foto, of alle foto's, op elk moment verwijderen. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.photos.q` | clinical | Do my photos leave my device? | Verlaten mijn foto's mijn apparaat? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreDown.a` | clinical | Most often: lighting, head angle, time of day, or fatigue. Sometimes: a genuinely harder week after illness or poor sleep. Occasionally: something worth a clinician looking at. A single lower scan is not evidence of decline. Two or three in a row is worth a conversation, and we will suggest one. | Meestal: het licht, de hoek van uw hoofd, het tijdstip of vermoeidheid. Soms: een echt zwaardere week na ziekte of slecht slapen. Af en toe: iets waar een behandelaar naar moet kijken. Eén lagere scan is geen bewijs van achteruitgang. Twee of drie op rij zijn een gesprek waard, en dat stellen we dan ook voor. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreDown.q` | clinical | My score went down. What does that mean? | Mijn score is gedaald. Wat betekent dat? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreMeaning.a` | clinical | It measures geometric symmetry, and nothing more. It is not a clinical severity grade, it does not translate to a House-Brackmann number, and it is not a percentage of recovery. Its only real job is comparing you today with you last month. Measurement variability is about ±2.5 points, so we treat smaller changes as noise. | Hij meet geometrische symmetrie, en verder niets. Het is geen klinische ernstgraad, hij is niet om te rekenen naar een House-Brackmann-score en het is geen herstelpercentage. Zijn enige echte taak is u vandaag vergelijken met u vorige maand. De meetvariatie is ongeveer ±2,5 punten, dus kleinere veranderingen beschouwen we als ruis. | FAQ answer. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreMeaning.q` | clinical | What does the score actually mean? | Wat betekent de score eigenlijk? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.title` | clinical | Scans and measurement | Scans en metingen |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.hero.lead` | clinical | Written by our clinical team, reviewed by a second clinician, and dated so you know how current they are. Where the evidence is thin, we say so. | Geschreven door ons klinische team, gecontroleerd door een tweede behandelaar en voorzien van een datum, zodat u weet hoe actueel ze zijn. Waar het bewijs mager is, zeggen we dat. | Evidence claim. |
| REVIEW | `guides.safety` | clinical | <b>If your eye is painful or red, or your vision has changed, see a doctor today.</b> Corneal damage from an eye that cannot close is the one complication of facial paralysis that can cause lasting harm, and it is treatable. That is more urgent than anything on this page. | <b>Is uw oog pijnlijk of rood, of is uw zicht veranderd? Ga dan vandaag nog naar een arts.</b> Schade aan het hoornvlies doordat het oog niet sluit, is de enige complicatie van aangezichtsverlamming die blijvende schade kan veroorzaken, en ze is te behandelen. Dat is dringender dan alles op deze pagina. | Corneal damage safety warning. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p1` | clinical | Sixteen questions, about eight minutes. Not a quiz you can pass — the answers set your starting point, so guessing at what sounds better makes your programme worse. | Zestien vragen, ongeveer acht minuten. Geen toets waarvoor u kunt slagen — de antwoorden bepalen uw startpunt, dus gokken naar wat beter klinkt, maakt uw programma slechter. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p2` | clinical | It asks when the weakness started, what your diagnosis is, what moves and what does not in three regions of your face, whether movements are linked to each other, and how daily life is going. It also asks about your eye in detail, because that is the part that can cause lasting harm. | We vragen wanneer de zwakte begon, welke diagnose u hebt, wat wel en niet beweegt in drie gebieden van uw gezicht, of bewegingen aan elkaar gekoppeld zijn, en hoe het dagelijks leven gaat. We vragen ook uitgebreid naar uw oog, omdat daar blijvende schade kan ontstaan. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p3` | clinical | If your answers show something that needs a doctor rather than an app — eye pain, weakness in a limb, weakness still worsening after weeks, both sides affected, or no diagnosis yet — we stop and say so. We would rather lose the sign-up. | Blijkt uit uw antwoorden iets waarvoor u een arts nodig hebt in plaats van een app — oogpijn, krachtverlies in een arm of been, zwakte die na weken nog toeneemt, beide kanten aangedaan, of nog geen diagnose — dan stoppen we en zeggen we dat. We verliezen liever de aanmelding. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.p1` | clinical | On the plans that include consultations, you book a 30-minute video call with a facial rehabilitation specialist and choose who you speak to. You can share your scans and reports with them beforehand, so the call is not spent on catching up. | Bij de abonnementen met consulten boekt u een videogesprek van 30 minuten met een specialist in revalidatie van het gezicht, en u kiest zelf met wie u spreekt. U kunt uw scans en rapporten vooraf met die persoon delen, zodat het gesprek niet opgaat aan bijpraten. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.p2` | clinical | Afterwards you get written notes and a specific plan. If your programme should change, they change it — a clinician can override the algorithm and often does. | Na afloop krijgt u schriftelijke aantekeningen en een concreet plan. Moet uw programma anders, dan past de behandelaar het aan — een behandelaar kan het algoritme overrulen en doet dat ook vaak. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.title` | clinical | The clinician | De behandelaar |  |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.p1` | clinical | You position your face in an outline and follow four prompts: rest, brow raise, gentle smile, eye closure. Around ninety seconds. | U plaatst uw gezicht in een omlijning en volgt vier aanwijzingen: in rust, wenkbrauwen optrekken, zachte glimlach, oog sluiten. Ongeveer negentig seconden. | Programme / scan / clinical process description. |
