| REVIEW | `guides.articles.stages-of-recovery.title` | clinical | The stages of recovery | Die fases van herstel | Guide titles and summaries. |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.what-is-pfp.blurb` | clinical | What has happened to the nerve, why one side of the face stopped moving, and what that does and does not mean. | Wat met die senuwee gebeur het, waarom een kant van die gesig opgehou het beweeg, en wat dit beteken en nie beteken nie. | Guide titles and summaries. |
| REVIEW | `guides.articles.what-is-pfp.title` | clinical | What is peripheral facial paralysis? | Wat is perifere gesigsverlamming? | Guide titles and summaries. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.cost.items.clinicians.a` | clinical | Licensed physiotherapists and physicians with specific training in facial rehabilitation. Their names, registrations, and languages are listed on our clinical team page. You can choose who you speak to. | Gelisensieerde fisioterapeute en dokters met spesifieke opleiding in gesigsrehabilitasie. Hul name, registrasies en tale word op ons bladsy oor die kliniese span gelys. U kan kies met wie u praat. | Clinician qualifications. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.diagnosis.a` | clinical | Yes. Palsya is a rehabilitation programme, not a diagnostic service. Facial weakness has causes that need medical investigation, some of them urgent. See a doctor first and come to us with a diagnosis. | Ja. Palsya is 'n rehabilitasieprogram, nie 'n diagnostiese diens nie. Swakheid in die gesig het oorsake wat mediese ondersoek nodig het, en sommige daarvan is dringend. Gaan eers na 'n dokter en kom na ons met 'n diagnose. | Diagnosis requirement. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.diagnosis.q` | clinical | Do I need a diagnosis first? | Het ek eers 'n diagnose nodig? | Diagnosis requirement. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.install.a` | clinical | No. Palsya runs in your browser on a phone, tablet, or computer. You will need to allow camera access for scans, and a reasonably steady internet connection. | Nee. Palsya werk in u blaaier op 'n foon, tablet of rekenaar. U sal kameratoegang vir skanderings moet toelaat, en u het 'n redelik stabiele internetverbinding nodig. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.install.q` | clinical | Do I need to install anything? | Moet ek iets installeer? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.rightForMe.a` | clinical | Palsya is built for people with peripheral facial paralysis — Bell's palsy, Ramsay Hunt, and post-surgical or post-traumatic facial nerve injury — whose onset was between 5 and 90 days ago. If your onset was more recent than 5 days, you should be under medical assessment first; steroids given early make a measurable difference and that window matters more than starting rehabilitation. If it was longer than 90 days ago, a structured programme can still help, but you should speak to a clinician about the right starting point. Our assessment will tell you honestly if we are not the right fit. | Palsya is gebou vir mense met perifere gesigsverlamming — Bell se verlamming, Ramsay Hunt, en gesigsenuweebesering ná 'n operasie of trauma — wie se aanvang tussen 5 en 90 dae gelede was. As u aanvang minder as 5 dae gelede was, moet u eers medies ondersoek word; steroïede wat vroeg gegee word, maak 'n meetbare verskil, en daardie tydsvenster is belangriker as om met rehabilitasie te begin. As dit meer as 90 dae gelede was, kan 'n gestruktureerde program steeds help, maar u moet met 'n klinikus praat oor die regte beginpunt. Ons assessering sal u eerlik sê as ons nie by u pas nie. | Eligibility (5-90 days), steroids. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.rightForMe.q` | clinical | Is Palsya right for me? | Is Palsya geskik vir my? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.technology.a` | clinical | The daily session is one screen with one button. Scans are guided step by step with plain instructions. If you get stuck, support answers in your language and can walk you through it on a call. | Die daaglikse sessie is een skerm met een knoppie. Skanderings word stap vir stap met eenvoudige instruksies begelei. As u vasval, antwoord ondersteuning in u taal en kan hulle u tydens 'n oproep daardeur lei. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.items.technology.q` | clinical | What if I am not confident with technology? | Wat as ek nie selfversekerd met tegnologie is nie? |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.gettingStarted.title` | clinical | Getting started | Aan die gang kom |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.missDays.a` | clinical | Nothing happens. Your programme does not reset, nothing is lost, and we will not send you guilt. Open the app and do today's session. If a pattern of missed days appears, your clinician may ask about it — usually because the programme needs adjusting, not because you do. | Niks gebeur nie. U program word nie teruggestel nie, niks gaan verlore nie, en ons sal u nie skuldig laat voel nie. Maak die toep oop en doen vandag se sessie. As 'n patroon van gemiste dae verskyn, kan u klinikus daaroor vra — gewoonlik omdat die program aangepas moet word, nie u nie. | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.missDays.q` | clinical | What if I miss days? | Wat as ek dae mis? | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.physio.a` | clinical | Yes, and many people do. Tell your therapist you are using Palsya and share your progress reports with them. If their guidance conflicts with ours, follow theirs — they can see you. | Ja, en baie mense doen dit. Sê vir u terapeut dat u Palsya gebruik en deel u vorderingsverslae met hulle. As hul leiding met ons s'n bots, volg hulle s'n — hulle kan u sien. | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.physio.q` | clinical | Can I use this alongside in-person physiotherapy? | Kan ek dit saam met persoonlike fisioterapie gebruik? | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.recovery.a` | clinical | We will not answer that, because nobody honestly can. Most people with Bell's palsy recover well. A minority retain lasting asymmetry. We will not predict which group you are in, and you should be wary of anyone who does. | Ons sal dit nie beantwoord nie, want niemand kan dit eerlik doen nie. Die meeste mense met Bell se verlamming herstel goed. 'n Minderheid behou blywende asimmetrie. Ons sal nie voorspel in watter groep u is nie, en u moet versigtig wees vir enigiemand wat dit doen. | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.recovery.q` | clinical | Will I definitely recover? | Sal ek beslis herstel? | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.timePerDay.a` | clinical | Between 10 and 20 minutes depending on your stage, usually split into two shorter sessions. Stage 1 is the shortest because it is mostly protection. Stage 3 is the longest because coordination work needs rest between repetitions. | Tussen 10 en 20 minute, afhangende van u fase, gewoonlik verdeel in twee korter sessies. Fase 1 is die kortste omdat dit meestal beskerming is. Fase 3 is die langste omdat koördinasiewerk rus tussen herhalings nodig het. | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.items.timePerDay.q` | clinical | How much time does it take each day? | Hoeveel tyd neem dit elke dag? | Programme and recovery claims. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.programme.title` | clinical | The programme | Die program |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.howScan.a` | clinical | You position your face in an outline and follow four prompts — rest, brow raise, gentle smile, eye closure. Facial landmarks are detected and the geometry of your affected side is compared with the mirrored unaffected side. The result is a symmetry score out of 100 relative to your own baseline. | U plaas u gesig binne 'n buitelyn en volg vier opdragte — rus, wenkbroue lig, sagte glimlag, oë toemaak. Gesigslandmerke word bepaal en die geometrie van u aangetaste kant word vergelyk met die gespieëlde onaangetaste kant. Die resultaat is 'n simmetrietelling uit 100 relatief tot u eie basislyn. | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.howScan.q` | clinical | How does the scan work? | Hoe werk die skandering? | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.photos.a` | clinical | Landmark detection runs on your device — the images themselves are not sent anywhere for processing. Your photos are stored only if you consent to it, and only so you can see your own timeline. You can delete any photo, or all of them, at any time. | Landmerkbepaling gebeur op u toestel — die beelde self word nêrens heen gestuur vir verwerking nie. U foto's word slegs gestoor as u daartoe instem, en slegs sodat u u eie tydlyn kan sien. U kan enige foto, of almal, op enige tyd uitvee. | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.photos.q` | clinical | Do my photos leave my device? | Verlaat my foto's my toestel? | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreDown.a` | clinical | Most often: lighting, head angle, time of day, or fatigue. Sometimes: a genuinely harder week after illness or poor sleep. Occasionally: something worth a clinician looking at. A single lower scan is not evidence of decline. Two or three in a row is worth a conversation, and we will suggest one. | Meestal: beligting, hoek van die kop, tyd van die dag, of moegheid. Soms: 'n werklik moeiliker week ná siekte of swak slaap. Af en toe: iets waarna 'n klinikus moet kyk. 'n Enkele laer skandering is nie 'n bewys van agteruitgang nie. Twee of drie agtereenvolgens is 'n gesprek werd, en ons sal een voorstel. | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreDown.q` | clinical | My score went down. What does that mean? | My telling het gedaal. Wat beteken dit? | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreMeaning.q` | clinical | What does the score actually mean? | Wat beteken die telling eintlik? | Scan/score meaning. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.title` | clinical | Scans and measurement | Skanderings en meting |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.hero.lead` | clinical | Written by our clinical team, reviewed by a second clinician, and dated so you know how current they are. Where the evidence is thin, we say so. | Geskryf deur ons kliniese span, hersien deur 'n tweede klinikus, en gedateer sodat u weet hoe onlangs hulle is. Waar die bewyse dun is, sê ons so. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.safety` | clinical | <b>If your eye is painful or red, or your vision has changed, see a doctor today.</b> Corneal damage from an eye that cannot close is the one complication of facial paralysis that can cause lasting harm, and it is treatable. That is more urgent than anything on this page. | <b>As u oog pynlik of rooi is, of u sig verander het, gaan vandag na 'n dokter.</b> Skade aan die kornea van 'n oog wat nie kan toemaak nie, is die een komplikasie van gesigsverlamming wat blywende skade kan veroorsaak, en dit is behandelbaar. Dit is dringender as enigiets op hierdie bladsy. | Eye safety warning. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p1` | clinical | Sixteen questions, about eight minutes. Not a quiz you can pass — the answers set your starting point, so guessing at what sounds better makes your programme worse. | Sestien vrae, sowat agt minute. Nie 'n vasvra wat u kan slaag nie — die antwoorde bepaal u beginpunt, so as u raai wat beter klink, word u program slegter. | Eligibility and red flags. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p2` | clinical | It asks when the weakness started, what your diagnosis is, what moves and what does not in three regions of your face, whether movements are linked to each other, and how daily life is going. It also asks about your eye in detail, because that is the part that can cause lasting harm. | Dit vra wanneer die swakheid begin het, wat u diagnose is, wat in drie areas van u gesig beweeg en wat nie, of bewegings aan mekaar gekoppel is, en hoe die daaglikse lewe gaan. Dit vra ook in besonderhede oor u oog, want dit is die deel wat blywende skade kan veroorsaak. | Eligibility and red flags. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.p3` | clinical | If your answers show something that needs a doctor rather than an app — eye pain, weakness in a limb, weakness still worsening after weeks, both sides affected, or no diagnosis yet — we stop and say so. We would rather lose the sign-up. | As u antwoorde iets toon wat 'n dokter eerder as 'n toep nodig het — oogpyn, swakheid in 'n arm of been, swakheid wat ná weke steeds erger word, albei kante wat aangetas is, of nog geen diagnose nie — hou ons op en sê dit. Ons verloor eerder die registrasie. | Eligibility and red flags. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.assessment.title` | clinical | The assessment | Die assessering | Eligibility and red flags. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.p1` | clinical | On the plans that include consultations, you book a 30-minute video call with a facial rehabilitation specialist and choose who you speak to. You can share your scans and reports with them beforehand, so the call is not spent on catching up. | Op die planne wat konsultasies insluit, bespreek u 'n video-oproep van 30 minute met 'n spesialis in gesigsrehabilitasie en kies u met wie u praat. U kan u skanderings en verslae vooraf met hulle deel, sodat die oproep nie aan bybring bestee word nie. | Clinician consultation claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.p2` | clinical | Afterwards you get written notes and a specific plan. If your programme should change, they change it — a clinician can override the algorithm and often does. | Daarna kry u geskrewe notas en 'n spesifieke plan. As u program moet verander, verander hulle dit — 'n klinikus kan die algoritme ignoreer en doen dit dikwels. | Clinician consultation claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.clinician.title` | clinical | The clinician | Die klinikus | Clinician consultation claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.p1` | clinical | You position your face in an outline and follow four prompts: rest, brow raise, gentle smile, eye closure. Around ninety seconds. | U plaas u gesig binne 'n buitelyn en volg vier opdragte: rus, wenkbroue lig, sagte glimlag, oë toemaak. Sowat negentig sekondes. | Scan measurement claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.p2` | clinical | Before anything is measured, the image is checked for the things that produce false results — light coming from one side, too dark, blur, movement. If the image is not good enough we tell you and you retake it. A bad scan is worse than no scan. | Voordat enigiets gemeet word, word die beeld nagegaan vir die dinge wat valse resultate gee — lig wat van een kant af kom, te donker, onskerpte, beweging. As die beeld nie goed genoeg is nie, sê ons dit en u neem dit weer. 'n Swak skandering is erger as geen skandering nie. | Scan measurement claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.p3` | clinical | Landmarks are detected on your device. Your affected side is compared with the mirror image of your unaffected side, region by region, and combined into one score out of 100 relative to your own baseline. | Landmerke word op u toestel bepaal. U aangetaste kant word streek vir streek vergelyk met die spieëlbeeld van u onaangetaste kant, en saamgevoeg tot een telling uit 100 relatief tot u eie basislyn. | Scan measurement claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.title` | clinical | The weekly scan | Die weeklikse skandering | Scan measurement claims. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.session.p1` | clinical | One screen, one button. Three parts: taping, exercises, daily routine. Ten to twenty minutes depending on your stage, and you can split it into two. | Een skerm, een knoppie. Drie dele: kleefband, oefeninge, daaglikse roetine. Tien tot twintig minute, afhangende van u fase, en u kan dit in twee verdeel. |  |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.session.p2` | clinical | Every exercise is guided by a timer with spoken-style cues, a diagram, the repetition count for your stage, and the one thing most people get wrong about that particular movement. Taping shows you where each strip goes and how much tension — which is the mistake almost everyone makes, always in the direction of too much. | Elke oefening word gelei deur 'n tydhouer met gesproke wenke, 'n diagram, die aantal herhalings vir u fase, en die een ding wat die meeste mense by daardie spesifieke beweging verkeerd doen. By die kleefband sien u waar elke strook gaan en hoeveel spanning — dit is die fout wat byna almal maak, altyd in die rigting van te veel. | Exercise/taping guidance claim. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.session.p3` | clinical | Mark it done and it is logged. Skip a day and nothing happens. | Merk dit as gedoen en dit word aangeteken. Slaan 'n dag oor en niks gebeur nie. |  |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.session.title` | clinical | The daily session | Die daaglikse sessie |  |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.stage.p1` | clinical | Your answers go through a placement process built by our clinical team: time since onset sets an outer bound, what actually moves does the real work, then modifiers for linked movement and tightness, then a confidence check. | U antwoorde gaan deur 'n indelingsproses wat deur ons kliniese span gebou is: die tyd sedert aanvang stel 'n buitegrens, wat werklik beweeg doen die eintlike werk, dan volg aanpassings vir gekoppelde beweging en styfheid, en dan 'n betroubaarheidstoets. | Placement process and clinician review. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.stage.p2` | clinical | You see the reasoning, in plain language. Not "Stage 3" on its own, but why Stage 3 and what specifically that changes about your day. | U sien die redenasie, in gewone taal. Nie net "Fase 3" nie, maar waarom fase 3 en wat dit spesifiek aan u dag verander. | Placement process and clinician review. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.stage.p3` | clinical | When the answers do not sit together in a usual pattern, or your onset was more than 90 days ago, or your paralysis followed surgery or an injury, a clinician reviews the placement before your programme starts. That review is within one working day and it is included. | Wanneer die antwoorde nie in 'n gewone patroon saampas nie, of u aanvang meer as 90 dae gelede was, of u verlamming ná 'n operasie of 'n besering gevolg het, hersien 'n klinikus die indeling voordat u program begin. Daardie hersiening gebeur binne een werksdag en is ingesluit. | Placement process and clinician review. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.stage.title` | clinical | Your stage, and why | U fase, en waarom | Placement process and clinician review. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.timeline.p1` | clinical | A score timeline with the measurement margin drawn on it, so you can see which changes are real and which are noise. A side-by-side comparison with any earlier scan. A photo timeline you control and can delete at any point. | 'n Tydlyn van u telling met die meetmarge daarop aangedui, sodat u kan sien watter veranderinge werklik is en watter ruis is. 'n Vergelyking langs mekaar met enige vroeëre skandering. 'n Fototydlyn wat u beheer en op enige stadium kan uitvee. |  |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.timeline.p2` | clinical | Progress reports written from your own data, in a template that will not predict your outcome or praise you for effort. Milestones that are factual observations — "lip seal held while drinking" — not badges. | Vorderingsverslae wat uit u eie data geskryf word, in 'n sjabloon wat nie u uitkoms sal voorspel of u vir inspanning sal prys nie. Mylpale wat feitelike waarnemings is — "lippe het tydens drink toe gebly" — nie kentekens nie. |  |
| REVIEW | `landing.ethics.badges.body` | clinical | You are recovering from a nerve injury, not playing a game. Progress is shown as measurements and as things you can newly do. | U herstel van 'n senuweebesering, u speel nie 'n speletjie nie. Vordering word gewys as metings en as dinge wat u nuut kan doen. |  |
| REVIEW | `landing.ethics.predictions.body` | clinical | We will never tell you when your face will recover or how much of it will come back. Nobody can know that from your data, and being told a number you then miss does real harm. | Ons sal u nooit sê wanneer u gesig sal herstel of hoeveel daarvan sal terugkom nie. Niemand kan dit uit u data weet nie, en om 'n getal te hoor wat u dan nie haal nie, doen werklike skade. | No recovery predictions. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.assessment.body` | clinical | Sixteen questions about what moves, what does not, and what daily life is like right now. It also checks that we are the right fit — and tells you plainly if we are not. | Sestien vrae oor wat beweeg, wat nie, en hoe die daaglikse lewe nou lyk. Dit gaan ook na of ons by u pas — en sê u reguit as ons nie pas nie. | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.assessment.title` | clinical | Answer the assessment | Beantwoord die assessering | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.daily.body` | clinical | Taping, exercises, and the daily routine, guided step by step. A scan once a week. That is the whole commitment. | Kleefband, oefeninge en die daaglikse roetine, stap vir stap begelei. Een skandering per week. Dit is die hele verbintenis. | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.daily.title` | clinical | Ten to twenty minutes a day | Tien tot twintig minute per dag | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.eyebrow` | clinical | Getting started | Aan die gang kom | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.plan.body` | clinical | You are placed in one of five stages with the reasoning shown. If your answers are unusual, a clinician reviews the placement before you start. | U word in een van vyf fases ingedeel, met die redenasie gewys. As u antwoorde ongewoon is, hersien 'n klinikus die indeling voordat u begin. | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.plan.title` | clinical | Get your stage and your plan | Kry u fase en u plan | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.gettingStarted.title` | clinical | Fifteen minutes to set up | Vyftien minute om op te stel | Assessment and placement claims. |
| REVIEW | `landing.hero.lead` | clinical | A structured daily programme built by facial rehabilitation specialists — taping, exercises, and eye care matched to the stage your recovery is actually in. With scans that show you change you cannot see in the mirror. | 'n Gestruktureerde daaglikse program, gebou deur spesialiste in gesigsrehabilitasie — kleefband, oefeninge en oogsorg wat pas by die fase waarin u herstel werklik is. Met skanderings wat u verandering wys wat u nie in die spieël kan sien nie. | Clinical claim. |
| REVIEW | `landing.meta.description` | clinical | A structured daily programme built by facial rehabilitation specialists — taping, exercises, and eye care matched to the stage your recovery is actually in. | 'n Gestruktureerde daaglikse program, gebou deur spesialiste in gesigsrehabilitasie — kleefband, oefeninge en oogsorg wat pas by die fase waarin u herstel werklik is. |  |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.eyebrow` | clinical | Why we built this | Waarom ons dit gebou het | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.lead` | clinical | A photocopied sheet of exercises, no idea which ones apply to you this week, no way to tell whether anything is working, and four months to wait before your next appointment. That gap is where Palsya lives. | 'n Gefotostateerde blad met oefeninge, geen idee watter daarvan hierdie week op u van toepassing is nie, geen manier om te weet of iets werk nie, en vier maande se wag tot u volgende afspraak. Palsya bestaan in daardie gaping. | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.person.body` | clinical | Video consultations with facial rehabilitation specialists. Most people are doing one or two things wrong; a clinician usually spots it in the first ten minutes. | Videokonsultasies met spesialiste in gesigsrehabilitasie. Die meeste mense doen een of twee dinge verkeerd; 'n klinikus sien dit gewoonlik in die eerste tien minute raak. | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.person.title` | clinical | A person who has seen this before | Iemand wat dit al voorheen gesien het | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.proof.body` | clinical | A weekly scan measures the geometry of your face against your own baseline. During the plateau weeks — and there are plateau weeks — this is what tells you the truth. | 'n Weeklikse skandering meet die geometrie van u gesig teenoor u eie basislyn. In die plato-weke — en daar is plato-weke — is dit wat u die waarheid vertel. | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.proof.title` | clinical | Proof you are not imagining it | Bewys dat u dit nie verbeel nie | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.rightWork.body` | clinical | Forcing a big smile helps in week six and holds you back in week two. Your programme is matched to the stage your recovery is in and changes as that changes. | Om 'n groot glimlag te forseer, help in week ses en hou u terug in week twee. U program pas by die fase waarin u herstel is en verander soos dit verander. | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.rightWork.title` | clinical | The right work for this week | Die regte werk vir hierdie week | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `landing.problem.title` | clinical | Most people are sent home with a leaflet | Die meeste mense word met 'n pamflet huis toe gestuur | Claims about stage-matched work and scans. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.instructions` | clinical | Sit facing a mirror, shoulders down, jaw loose. Place two fingers flat on the <b>unaffected</b> eyebrow and hold it still. This stops the good side doing the work. Raise the affected eyebrow as if mildly surprised. Aim for a small movement you can control, not the biggest you can manage. Hold for three seconds, then let it fall on its own. Do not push it down. Wait five seconds between repetitions. | Sit voor 'n spieël, skouers laag, kakebeen los. Plaas twee vingers plat op die <b>onaangetaste</b> wenkbrou en hou dit stil. Dit keer dat die goeie kant die werk doen. Lig die aangetaste wenkbrou asof u effens verras is. Mik na 'n klein beweging wat u kan beheer, nie die grootste wat u kan regkry nie. Hou vir drie sekondes, en laat dit dan vanself sak. Moenie dit afdruk nie. Wag vyf sekondes tussen herhalings. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.name` | clinical | Brow lift | Wenkbroulig | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.steps.s1` | clinical | Sit facing a mirror, shoulders down, jaw loose. | Sit voor 'n spieël, skouers laag, kakebeen los. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.steps.s2` | clinical | Place two fingers flat on the <b>unaffected</b> eyebrow and hold it still. This stops the good side doing the work. | Plaas twee vingers plat op die <b>onaangetaste</b> wenkbrou en hou dit stil. Dit keer dat die goeie kant die werk doen. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.steps.s3` | clinical | Raise the affected eyebrow as if mildly surprised. Aim for a small movement you can control, not the biggest you can manage. | Lig die aangetaste wenkbrou asof u effens verras is. Mik na 'n klein beweging wat u kan beheer, nie die grootste wat u kan regkry nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.steps.s4` | clinical | Hold for three seconds, then let it fall on its own. Do not push it down. | Hou vir drie sekondes, en laat dit dan vanself sak. Moenie dit afdruk nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.steps.s5` | clinical | Wait five seconds between repetitions. | Wag vyf sekondes tussen herhalings. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.watch` | clinical | If the eye closes or the mouth pulls when you lift, use less effort. That linkage gets stronger with practice, so we practise below it. | As die oog toegaan of die mond trek wanneer u lig, gebruik minder inspanning. Daardie koppeling word sterker met oefening, daarom oefen ons onder daardie drempel. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.brow.why` | clinical | The brow is often the last region to return. Working it keeps the pathway active and prevents the forehead from becoming stiff on the affected side. | Die wenkbrou is dikwels die laaste area wat terugkom. Deur dit te oefen, bly die senuweebaan aktief en word voorkom dat die voorkop aan die aangetaste kant styf word. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.instructions` | clinical | Take a normal breath and puff both cheeks with the lips closed. Hold for five seconds without letting air escape. If air escapes on the affected side, hold that cheek lightly with a clean hand and try again. Release slowly through the nose, not with a pop. | Haal normaal asem en blaas albei wange op met die lippe toe. Hou vir vyf sekondes sonder om lug te laat ontsnap. As lug aan die aangetaste kant ontsnap, hou daardie wang liggies met 'n skoon hand vas en probeer weer. Laat die lug stadig deur die neus uit, nie met 'n klap nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.name` | clinical | Cheek puff with hold | Wange opblaas en hou | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.steps.s1` | clinical | Take a normal breath and puff both cheeks with the lips closed. | Haal normaal asem en blaas albei wange op met die lippe toe. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.steps.s2` | clinical | Hold for five seconds without letting air escape. | Hou vir vyf sekondes sonder om lug te laat ontsnap. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.steps.s3` | clinical | If air escapes on the affected side, hold that cheek lightly with a clean hand and try again. | As lug aan die aangetaste kant ontsnap, hou daardie wang liggies met 'n skoon hand vas en probeer weer. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.steps.s4` | clinical | Release slowly through the nose, not with a pop. | Laat die lug stadig deur die neus uit, nie met 'n klap nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.why` | clinical | Air retention tests the seal under load and builds the endurance you need for speech and eating rather than for exercises. | Om lug in te hou, toets die seël onder las en bou die uithouvermoë wat u vir praat en eet nodig het, eerder as vir oefeninge. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.instructions` | clinical | Look straight ahead. Let the face rest first. Close both eyes softly, as if falling asleep — not squeezing shut. Hold for two seconds and notice whether the affected lid has met the lower lid. Open slowly and completely. If the lid does not fully close, do not force it. Close as far as it goes and support the lid gently with a clean fingertip. | Kyk reguit vorentoe. Laat die gesig eers rus. Maak albei oë sag toe, asof u aan die slaap raak — nie styf toeknyp nie. Hou vir twee sekondes en let op of die aangetaste ooglid die onderste ooglid geraak het. Maak stadig en heeltemal oop. As die ooglid nie heeltemal toegaan nie, moenie dit forseer nie. Maak so ver toe as wat dit gaan en ondersteun die ooglid saggies met 'n skoon vingerpunt. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.name` | clinical | Gentle eye closure | Sagte oogsluiting | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.steps.s1` | clinical | Look straight ahead. Let the face rest first. | Kyk reguit vorentoe. Laat die gesig eers rus. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.steps.s2` | clinical | Close both eyes softly, as if falling asleep — not squeezing shut. | Maak albei oë sag toe, asof u aan die slaap raak — nie styf toeknyp nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.steps.s3` | clinical | Hold for two seconds and notice whether the affected lid has met the lower lid. | Hou vir twee sekondes en let op of die aangetaste ooglid die onderste ooglid geraak het. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.steps.s4` | clinical | Open slowly and completely. | Maak stadig en heeltemal oop. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.steps.s5` | clinical | If the lid does not fully close, do not force it. Close as far as it goes and support the lid gently with a clean fingertip. | As die ooglid nie heeltemal toegaan nie, moenie dit forseer nie. Maak so ver toe as wat dit gaan en ondersteun die ooglid saggies met 'n skoon vingerpunt. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.watch` | clinical | Forceful squeezing recruits the mouth and the neck. If you feel the corner of your mouth move, you are squeezing. | Kragtige toeknyp span die mond en die nek in. As u voel dat u mondhoek beweeg, knyp u te hard. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.eye.why` | clinical | Closure protects the cornea. Gentle repeated closure also restores the blink pattern, which recovers separately from forceful closing. | Sluiting beskerm die kornea. Sagte, herhaalde sluiting herstel ook die knippatroon, wat afsonderlik van kragtige toeknyp herstel. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.instructions` | clinical | Breathe in through the nose and let the nostrils widen as you do. Try to widen the nostril on the affected side slightly more. Hold two seconds, release fully. Keep the upper lip still — this is a nose movement, not a sneer. | Asem deur die neus in en laat die neusgate wyer word terwyl u dit doen. Probeer die neusgat aan die aangetaste kant effens meer verwyd. Hou twee sekondes, laat heeltemal los. Hou die bolip stil — dit is 'n neusbeweging, nie 'n smalende trek nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.steps.s1` | clinical | Breathe in through the nose and let the nostrils widen as you do. | Asem deur die neus in en laat die neusgate wyer word terwyl u dit doen. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.steps.s2` | clinical | Try to widen the nostril on the affected side slightly more. | Probeer die neusgat aan die aangetaste kant effens meer verwyd. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.steps.s3` | clinical | Hold two seconds, release fully. | Hou twee sekondes, laat heeltemal los. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.steps.s4` | clinical | Keep the upper lip still — this is a nose movement, not a sneer. | Hou die bolip stil — dit is 'n neusbeweging, nie 'n smalende trek nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.watch` | clinical | Keep the top teeth covered. If the lip lifts to show teeth, the wrong muscle has taken over. | Hou die boonste tande bedek. As die lip oplig sodat tande wys, het die verkeerde spier oorgeneem. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.instructions` | clinical | Bring the lips together and forward, as if about to say "ooh". Keep the pucker centred — do not let it drift towards the stronger side. Hold three seconds. Press the lips together lightly, no clenching. Release and let the lips part. | Bring die lippe saam en vorentoe, asof u "oe" gaan sê. Hou die tuit in die middel — moenie dit na die sterker kant laat skuif nie. Hou drie sekondes. Druk die lippe liggies op mekaar, sonder om te klem. Laat los en laat die lippe oopgaan. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.name` | clinical | Lip pucker and seal | Lippe tuit en seël | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.steps.s1` | clinical | Bring the lips together and forward, as if about to say "ooh". | Bring die lippe saam en vorentoe, asof u "oe" gaan sê. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.steps.s2` | clinical | Keep the pucker centred — do not let it drift towards the stronger side. | Hou die tuit in die middel — moenie dit na die sterker kant laat skuif nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.steps.s3` | clinical | Hold three seconds. Press the lips together lightly, no clenching. | Hou drie sekondes. Druk die lippe liggies op mekaar, sonder om te klem. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.steps.s4` | clinical | Release and let the lips part. | Laat los en laat die lippe oopgaan. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.watch` | clinical | If the pucker pulls sideways, place a fingertip lightly at the strong corner to keep it honest. | As die tuit sywaarts trek, plaas 'n vingerpunt liggies by die sterk mondhoek om dit eerlik te hou. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.pucker.why` | clinical | Lip seal is what lets you drink, eat, and speak plosive sounds. It is often the first change patients notice in daily life. | Die lipseël is wat u in staat stel om te drink, te eet en plofklanke te sê. Dit is dikwels die eerste verandering wat pasiënte in die daaglikse lewe opmerk. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.instructions` | clinical | Place clean fingertips on the tight area — commonly the cheek or the side of the chin. Apply light, steady pressure and stretch the skin slowly in the direction opposite to the tightness. Hold fifteen seconds, breathing normally. It should feel like a stretch, never a sharp pain. Release slowly and let the tissue settle before repeating. | Plaas skoon vingerpunte op die stywe area — gewoonlik die wang of die kant van die ken. Druk liggies en egalig en rek die vel stadig in die rigting teenoor die styfheid. Hou vyftien sekondes en asem normaal. Dit moet soos 'n strek voel, nooit soos 'n skerp pyn nie. Laat stadig los en laat die weefsel bedaar voordat u herhaal. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.name` | clinical | Tightness release | Styfheid losmaak | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.steps.s1` | clinical | Place clean fingertips on the tight area — commonly the cheek or the side of the chin. | Plaas skoon vingerpunte op die stywe area — gewoonlik die wang of die kant van die ken. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.steps.s2` | clinical | Apply light, steady pressure and stretch the skin slowly in the direction opposite to the tightness. | Druk liggies en egalig en rek die vel stadig in die rigting teenoor die styfheid. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.steps.s3` | clinical | Hold fifteen seconds, breathing normally. It should feel like a stretch, never a sharp pain. | Hou vyftien sekondes en asem normaal. Dit moet soos 'n strek voel, nooit soos 'n skerp pyn nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.steps.s4` | clinical | Release slowly and let the tissue settle before repeating. | Laat stadig los en laat die weefsel bedaar voordat u herhaal. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.why` | clinical | Later in recovery, tightness limits movement more than weakness does. Release work restores range that activation work cannot reach. | Later in die herstel beperk styfheid beweging meer as swakheid. Loslaatwerk herstel omvang wat aktiveringswerk nie kan bereik nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.instructions` | clinical | Watch the mirror. Smile gently — a third of maximum — while consciously keeping both eyes wide open. If the eye narrows at all, reduce the smile until it does not. Hold four seconds with the eye open, then release. Now reverse it: close the eyes softly while keeping the mouth completely still. Rest eight seconds between repetitions. This drill fatigues quickly. | Kyk in die spieël. Glimlag saggies — 'n derde van die maksimum — terwyl u bewustelik albei oë wyd oophou. As die oog enigsins vernou, verklein die glimlag totdat dit nie meer gebeur nie. Hou vier sekondes met die oog oop, en laat dan los. Doen dit nou andersom: maak die oë sag toe terwyl u die mond heeltemal stilhou. Rus agt sekondes tussen herhalings. Hierdie oefening maak gou moeg. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.name` | clinical | Eye–mouth separation | Skeiding van oog en mond | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.steps.s1` | clinical | Watch the mirror. Smile gently — a third of maximum — while consciously keeping both eyes wide open. | Kyk in die spieël. Glimlag saggies — 'n derde van die maksimum — terwyl u bewustelik albei oë wyd oophou. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.steps.s2` | clinical | If the eye narrows at all, reduce the smile until it does not. | As die oog enigsins vernou, verklein die glimlag totdat dit nie meer gebeur nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.steps.s3` | clinical | Hold four seconds with the eye open, then release. | Hou vier sekondes met die oog oop, en laat dan los. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.steps.s4` | clinical | Now reverse it: close the eyes softly while keeping the mouth completely still. | Doen dit nou andersom: maak die oë sag toe terwyl u die mond heeltemal stilhou. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.steps.s5` | clinical | Rest eight seconds between repetitions. This drill fatigues quickly. | Rus agt sekondes tussen herhalings. Hierdie oefening maak gou moeg. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.watch` | clinical | This is the exercise where less is genuinely more. Five accurate repetitions beat twenty linked ones. | Dit is die oefening waar minder werklik meer is. Vyf akkurate herhalings is beter as twintig gekoppelde. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.separate.why` | clinical | This is the core drill against synkinesis — the linked movement where one region fires when you ask for another. Separation is trained slowly and at low effort. | Dit is die kernoefening teen sinkinese — die gekoppelde beweging waar een area saamtrek wanneer u 'n ander een wil gebruik. Skeiding word stadig en met min inspanning geoefen. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.instructions` | clinical | Rest your lips together, teeth apart. Draw both corners of the mouth up and slightly outward — a closed-lip smile, roughly a third of your maximum. Watch the mirror: are the corners moving the same distance? If not, use less effort until they are. Hold three seconds, release slowly over two seconds. Let the face go completely blank between repetitions. | Laat u lippe op mekaar rus, tande effens oop. Trek albei mondhoeke op en effens na buite — 'n glimlag met geslote lippe, ongeveer 'n derde van u maksimum. Kyk in die spieël: beweeg die mondhoeke ewe ver? Indien nie, gebruik minder inspanning totdat hulle dit doen. Hou drie sekondes, laat stadig oor twee sekondes los. Laat die gesig tussen herhalings heeltemal uitdrukkingloos word. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.name` | clinical | Gentle smile | Sagte glimlag | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.steps.s1` | clinical | Rest your lips together, teeth apart. | Laat u lippe op mekaar rus, tande effens oop. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.steps.s2` | clinical | Draw both corners of the mouth up and slightly outward — a closed-lip smile, roughly a third of your maximum. | Trek albei mondhoeke op en effens na buite — 'n glimlag met geslote lippe, ongeveer 'n derde van u maksimum. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.steps.s3` | clinical | Watch the mirror: are the corners moving the same distance? If not, use less effort until they are. | Kyk in die spieël: beweeg die mondhoeke ewe ver? Indien nie, gebruik minder inspanning totdat hulle dit doen. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.steps.s4` | clinical | Hold three seconds, release slowly over two seconds. | Hou drie sekondes, laat stadig oor twee sekondes los. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.steps.s5` | clinical | Let the face go completely blank between repetitions. | Laat die gesig tussen herhalings heeltemal uitdrukkingloos word. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.watch` | clinical | The eye narrowing as you smile is the linkage we are working against. Less effort, not more repetitions. | Die oog wat vernou terwyl u glimlag, is die koppeling waarteen ons werk. Minder inspanning, nie meer herhalings nie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.smile.why` | clinical | The most functionally important and the most commonly overdone. A controlled small smile trains the pattern; a forced big smile trains the compensation. | Die belangrikste vir funksie en die een wat die meeste oordoen word. 'n Beheerde klein glimlag oefen die patroon; 'n geforseerde groot glimlag oefen die kompensasie. | Exercise instructions/cautions. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.body` | clinical | Food collecting in the affected cheek and drinks escaping the lip seal are mechanical problems with mechanical answers. Smaller mouthfuls, food towards the stronger side, a mirror check after eating, and a straw only if it does not make the seal harder. | Kos wat in die aangetaste wang versamel en drank wat by die lipseël ontsnap, is meganiese probleme met meganiese oplossings. Kleiner happies, kos na die sterker kant, 'n spieëlkontrole ná ete, en 'n strooitjie slegs as dit die seël nie moeiliker maak nie. | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.instructions` | clinical | Smaller mouthfuls, slower pace Place food towards the unaffected side Rinse and check the affected cheek after eating Sit upright while drinking | Kleiner happies, stadiger tempo Plaas kos na die onaangetaste kant Spoel en kontroleer die aangetaste wang ná ete Sit regop terwyl u drink | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.items.i1` | clinical | Smaller mouthfuls, slower pace | Kleiner happies, stadiger tempo | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.items.i2` | clinical | Place food towards the unaffected side | Plaas kos na die onaangetaste kant | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.items.i3` | clinical | Rinse and check the affected cheek after eating | Spoel en kontroleer die aangetaste wang ná ete | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.items.i4` | clinical | Sit upright while drinking | Sit regop terwyl u drink | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.name` | clinical | Eating and drinking | Eet en drink | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eat.when` | clinical | Every meal | Elke ete | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.body` | clinical | If your eye does not close fully, protecting the cornea is the single most important thing in your day — more important than any exercise. Preservative-free lubricating drops during the day, ointment at night, and a check with your clinician if the eye becomes red, painful, or your vision changes. | As u oog nie heeltemal toegaan nie, is die beskerming van die kornea die heel belangrikste ding in u dag — belangriker as enige oefening. Bevogtigende oogdruppels sonder preserveermiddels gedurende die dag, oogsalf in die nag, en raadpleeg u klinikus as die oog rooi of pynlik word, of as u sig verander. | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.instructions` | clinical | Lubricating drops on waking Drops every 2–3 hours while awake Ointment and taped closure or a moisture chamber at night Sunglasses outdoors, including on cloudy days | Bevogtigende oogdruppels wanneer u wakker word Druppels elke 2–3 uur terwyl u wakker is Oogsalf en die ooglid met kleefband toegeplak, of 'n vogkamer, in die nag Sonbril buite, ook op bewolkte dae | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.items.i1` | clinical | Lubricating drops on waking | Bevogtigende oogdruppels wanneer u wakker word | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.items.i2` | clinical | Drops every 2–3 hours while awake | Druppels elke 2–3 uur terwyl u wakker is | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.items.i3` | clinical | Ointment and taped closure or a moisture chamber at night | Oogsalf en die ooglid met kleefband toegeplak, of 'n vogkamer, in die nag | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.items.i4` | clinical | Sunglasses outdoors, including on cloudy days | Sonbril buite, ook op bewolkte dae | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.name` | clinical | Eye care | Oogsorg | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.eye.when` | clinical | Morning, through the day, and before sleep | Soggens, deur die dag, en voor u gaan slaap | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.body` | clinical | Slow circular pressure with clean fingertips over the cheek, jaw, and around the mouth. This maintains circulation and tissue mobility. It is not a workout — if it feels vigorous, ease off. | Stadige sirkelvormige druk met skoon vingerpunte oor die wang, kakebeen en rondom die mond. Dit handhaaf bloedsomloop en die beweeglikheid van die weefsel. Dit is nie 'n oefensessie nie — as dit kragtig voel, gaan ligter. | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.instructions` | clinical | Clean, dry hands Small circles, light pressure, upward direction Both sides, not only the affected side Stop if it causes twitching | Skoon, droë hande Klein sirkels, ligte druk, opwaartse rigting Albei kante, nie net die aangetaste kant nie Hou op as dit spiertrekkings veroorsaak | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.items.i1` | clinical | Clean, dry hands | Skoon, droë hande | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.items.i2` | clinical | Small circles, light pressure, upward direction | Klein sirkels, ligte druk, opwaartse rigting | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.items.i3` | clinical | Both sides, not only the affected side | Albei kante, nie net die aangetaste kant nie | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.items.i4` | clinical | Stop if it causes twitching | Hou op as dit spiertrekkings veroorsaak | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.name` | clinical | Massage | Masseer | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.massage.when` | clinical | Once or twice daily, 3–4 minutes | Een of twee keer per dag, 3–4 minute | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.body` | clinical | Facial fatigue is real and it flattens your movement quality. Sleep on your back or on the unaffected side so the face is not pressed into a pillow, and take the afternoon dip seriously — practise when you are fresh. | Moegheid in die gesig is werklik en dit verswak die gehalte van u beweging. Slaap op u rug of op die onaangetaste kant sodat u gesig nie in 'n kussing gedruk word nie, en neem die middagdip ernstig op — oefen wanneer u vars is. | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.instructions` | clinical | Back or unaffected side, not face-down Eye protection before lights out Practise in the morning if evenings are heavy Rest days are part of the programme | Op die rug of die onaangetaste kant, nie op die maag nie Oogbeskerming voordat die ligte afgaan Oefen soggens as die aande swaar is Rusdae is deel van die program | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.items.i1` | clinical | Back or unaffected side, not face-down | Op die rug of die onaangetaste kant, nie op die maag nie | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.items.i2` | clinical | Eye protection before lights out | Oogbeskerming voordat die ligte afgaan | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.items.i3` | clinical | Practise in the morning if evenings are heavy | Oefen soggens as die aande swaar is | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.items.i4` | clinical | Rest days are part of the programme | Rusdae is deel van die program | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.name` | clinical | Sleep and rest | Slaap en rus | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.sleep.when` | clinical | Nightly | Elke aand | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.instructions` | clinical | Read aloud for 3 minutes daily Emphasise p, b, m, f, v, w Slow enough to keep the seal Record yourself weekly if you find it useful | Lees daagliks 3 minute hardop Beklemtoon p, b, m, f, v, w Stadig genoeg om die seël te behou Neem uself weekliks op as u dit nuttig vind | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.items.i1` | clinical | Read aloud for 3 minutes daily | Lees daagliks 3 minute hardop | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.items.i3` | clinical | Slow enough to keep the seal | Stadig genoeg om die seël te behou | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.items.i4` | clinical | Record yourself weekly if you find it useful | Neem uself weekliks op as u dit nuttig vind | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.name` | clinical | Speech practice | Spraakoefening | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.when` | clinical | Once daily, 3 minutes | Een keer per dag, 3 minute | Daily routine: eye care, eating, massage, sleep, speech. |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.goal` | clinical | Reduce the effort a movement takes, so you can practise the correct pattern at low effort instead of straining into a wrong one. | Verminder die inspanning wat 'n beweging verg, sodat u die korrekte patroon met min inspanning kan oefen eerder as om in 'n verkeerde een in te forseer. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.instructions` | clinical | Zygomatic line: The affected corner of the mouth -> Along the natural smile line to just in front of the ear. Apply while the face is at rest, not while smiling. Brow: Just above the affected eyebrow, at the outer third -> Up towards the hairline. Keep well clear of the eye. If it pulls the lid, remove and reposition lower. | Zigomatiese lyn: Die aangetaste mondhoek -> Langs die natuurlike glimlaglyn tot net voor die oor. Plak terwyl die gesig rus, nie terwyl u glimlag nie. Wenkbrou: Net bo die aangetaste wenkbrou, by die buitenste derde -> Op na die haarlyn toe. Hou dit ver weg van die oog. As dit aan die ooglid trek, verwyder dit en plak dit laer. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.name` | clinical | Movement assist | Bewegingshulp | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s1.from` | clinical | The affected corner of the mouth | Die aangetaste mondhoek | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s1.note` | clinical | Apply while the face is at rest, not while smiling. | Plak terwyl die gesig rus, nie terwyl u glimlag nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s1.to` | clinical | Along the natural smile line to just in front of the ear | Langs die natuurlike glimlaglyn tot net voor die oor | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s2.from` | clinical | Just above the affected eyebrow, at the outer third | Net bo die aangetaste wenkbrou, by die buitenste derde | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s2.note` | clinical | Keep well clear of the eye. If it pulls the lid, remove and reposition lower. | Hou dit ver weg van die oog. As dit aan die ooglid trek, verwyder dit en plak dit laer. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s2.to` | clinical | Up towards the hairline | Op na die haarlyn toe | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s2.where` | clinical | Brow | Wenkbrou | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.tension` | clinical | Light to moderate — about 25% stretch across the middle of the strip. | Lig tot matig — sowat 25% rek oor die middel van die strook. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.wear` | clinical | During exercise sessions, plus up to 4 hours after. | Tydens oefensessies, plus tot 4 uur daarna. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.goal` | clinical | Give the skin a directional cue during coordination work and mildly discourage the linked movement you are trying to separate. | Gee die vel 'n rigtingwyser tydens koördinasiewerk en ontmoedig liggies die gekoppelde beweging wat u probeer skei. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.instructions` | clinical | Lower lid support: The outer corner of the eye -> Outward and slightly down, towards the temple. Never across the lid itself. Below the orbital rim only. Cheek counter-cue: Mid-cheek -> Downward and outward. This opposes the upward pull that comes with synkinesis. | Ondersteuning van die onderste ooglid: Die buitenste hoek van die oog -> Na buite en effens af, na die slaap toe. Nooit oor die ooglid self nie. Slegs onder die oogkasrand. Teenwenk op die wang: Middel van die wang -> Afwaarts en na buite. Dit werk die opwaartse trek teë wat met sinkinese gepaard gaan. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.name` | clinical | Directional guide | Rigtinggeleiding | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s1.from` | clinical | The outer corner of the eye | Die buitenste hoek van die oog | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s1.to` | clinical | Outward and slightly down, towards the temple | Na buite en effens af, na die slaap toe | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s1.where` | clinical | Lower lid support | Ondersteuning van die onderste ooglid | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s2.from` | clinical | Mid-cheek | Middel van die wang | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s2.note` | clinical | This opposes the upward pull that comes with synkinesis. | Dit werk die opwaartse trek teë wat met sinkinese gepaard gaan. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s2.to` | clinical | Downward and outward | Afwaarts en na buite | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.tension` | clinical | Light — about 15%. The cue is sensory, not mechanical. | Lig — sowat 15%. Die wenk is sensories, nie meganies nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.wear` | clinical | During coordination exercises only. 20–30 minutes. | Slegs tydens koördinasie-oefeninge. 20–30 minute. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.goal` | clinical | Hold the mid-face against gravity while the nerve is quiet, so the tissue does not stretch and set in a dropped position. | Hou die middelgesig teen swaartekrag terwyl die senuwee stil is, sodat die weefsel nie uitrek en in 'n afgesakte posisie vasgelê word nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.instructions` | clinical | Cheek: Just above the jawline, below the affected corner of the mouth -> Up and outward towards the cheekbone. Anchor with no stretch, apply the middle with light stretch, finish the last 2 cm with no stretch. Mouth corner: 1 cm out from the affected corner of the lip -> Diagonally up towards the ear. This one is very light. Its job is a cue, not a lift. | Wang: Net bo die kakebeenlyn, onder die aangetaste mondhoek -> Op en na buite na die wangbeen toe. Anker sonder rek, plak die middel met ligte rek, en plak die laaste 2 cm sonder rek. Mondhoek: 1 cm buite die aangetaste lipshoek -> Diagonaal op na die oor toe. Hierdie een is baie lig. Die doel is 'n wenk, nie 'n oplig nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.name` | clinical | Resting support | Rusondersteuning | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s1.from` | clinical | Just above the jawline, below the affected corner of the mouth | Net bo die kakebeenlyn, onder die aangetaste mondhoek | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s1.note` | clinical | Anchor with no stretch, apply the middle with light stretch, finish the last 2 cm with no stretch. | Anker sonder rek, plak die middel met ligte rek, en plak die laaste 2 cm sonder rek. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s1.to` | clinical | Up and outward towards the cheekbone | Op en na buite na die wangbeen toe | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s1.where` | clinical | Cheek | Wang | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s2.from` | clinical | 1 cm out from the affected corner of the lip | 1 cm buite die aangetaste lipshoek | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s2.note` | clinical | This one is very light. Its job is a cue, not a lift. | Hierdie een is baie lig. Die doel is 'n wenk, nie 'n oplig nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s2.to` | clinical | Diagonally up towards the ear | Diagonaal op na die oor toe | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.strips.s2.where` | clinical | Mouth corner | Mondhoek | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.tension` | clinical | Very light — about 10% stretch. You should be able to slide a fingertip underneath. | Baie lig — sowat 10% rek. U moet 'n vingerpunt daaronder kan inskuif. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.rest.wear` | clinical | 4–6 hours during the day. Never overnight. | 4–6 uur gedurende die dag. Nooit oornag nie. | Taping instructions (placement, tension, wear time). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.phase.blockedLead` | clinical | Something in your answers needs a doctor rather than a rehabilitation programme. We would rather lose your sign-up than have you start anyway. | Iets in u antwoorde het 'n dokter nodig eerder as 'n rehabilitasieprogram. Ons verloor eerder u registrasie as dat u in elk geval begin. | Blocked (safety) screen. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.phase.blockedSavedBody` | clinical | Nothing is lost. When you have been seen — or when enough time has passed — sign back in and your assessment will be where you left it. You can also read every guide in the knowledge base in the meantime, free and without an account. | Niks gaan verlore nie. Wanneer u gesien is — of wanneer genoeg tyd verloop het — meld weer aan en u assessering sal wees waar u dit gelos het. U kan intussen ook elke gids in die kennisbasis lees, gratis en sonder 'n rekening. | Blocked (safety) screen. |
