| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.sessionsFallback` | clinical | your daily session | u daaglikse sessie |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingSummary` | clinical | {name} — {minutes} minutes. | {name} — {minutes} minute. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingTitle` | clinical | Taping | Kleefband |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.trialTitle` | clinical | Your free 7 days start now | U gratis 7 dae begin nou |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.clinicalNote` | clinical | <b>These questions were written by our clinical team.</b> The placement they produce follows the same reasoning a physiotherapist uses in a first appointment: time since onset, then what actually moves, then whether movements are linked. | <b>Hierdie vrae is deur ons kliniese span geskryf.</b> Die indeling wat hulle oplewer, volg dieselfde redenasie wat 'n fisioterapeut in 'n eerste afspraak gebruik: die tyd sedert aanvang, dan wat werklik beweeg, dan of bewegings gekoppel is. | Clinical reasoning claim. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.heading` | clinical | Sixteen questions, about eight minutes | Sestien vrae, sowat agt minute |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.lead` | clinical | Your answers decide where your programme starts. There is no right answer and nothing to pass — an optimistic answer just gets you a programme that does not fit. | U antwoorde bepaal waar u program begin. Daar is geen regte antwoord nie en niks om te slaag nie — 'n optimistiese antwoord gee u net 'n program wat nie pas nie. |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.learnMore` | clinical | Read about the stages first | Lees eers oor die fases |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.mirrorBody` | clinical | Three questions ask you to try a movement and watch what happens. A bathroom mirror in even light is ideal. | Drie vrae vra dat u 'n beweging probeer en kyk wat gebeur. 'n Badkamerspieël in egalige lig is ideaal. | Assessment instruction. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.mirrorTitle` | clinical | Have a mirror nearby | Hou 'n spieël byderhand |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.safetyBody` | clinical | Some answers mean you need a doctor rather than an app. If that comes up we stop and say so, plainly. | Sommige antwoorde beteken dat u 'n dokter eerder as 'n toep nodig het. As dit gebeur, hou ons op en sê dit reguit. | Safety statement. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.safetyTitle` | clinical | We will tell you if we are wrong for you | Ons sal u sê as ons nie vir u geskik is nie |  |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.welcome.start` | clinical | Start the assessment | Begin die assessering |  |
| REVIEW | `pricing.extras.extension.body` | clinical | Extend month to month at {price}, or stop. Most people in Stage 4 or 5 extend for two or three months and then move to a short maintenance routine on their own. | Verleng maand vir maand teen {price}, of hou op. Die meeste mense in fase 4 of 5 verleng vir twee of drie maande en gaan dan op hul eie oor na 'n kort onderhoudsroetine. |  |
| REVIEW | `pricing.plans.guided.forWho` | clinical | The one most people choose. Two calls is usually enough to catch the mistakes that matter — taping tension, effort level, and synkinesis appearing. | Die een wat die meeste mense kies. Twee oproepe is gewoonlik genoeg om die foute raak te sien wat saak maak — kleefbandspanning, inspanningsvlak, en sinkinese wat begin verskyn. | Clinical mistakes (taping tension, synkinesis). |
| REVIEW | `pricing.plans.supported.forWho` | clinical | For more complex cases: significant synkinesis, post-surgical recovery, slow early progress, or anyone who simply wants regular contact with a person. | Vir meer komplekse gevalle: beduidende sinkinese, herstel ná 'n operasie, stadige vroeë vordering, of enigiemand wat bloot gereelde kontak met 'n mens wil hê. | Complex cases description. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a1` | clinical | Forced or maximum-effort movements | Geforseerde bewegings of bewegings met maksimum inspanning | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a2` | clinical | Electrical stimulation | Elektriese stimulasie | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a3` | clinical | Chewing gum as "exercise" | Kougom as "oefening" | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.avoid.a4` | clinical | Pushing through pain | Deur pyn heen druk | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.body` | clinical | Nothing you do with the muscle will speed the nerve up in this stage, and pushing hard can make things harder later. So the programme is deliberately quiet: protect the eye, keep the tissue supple, support the resting position of the face, and let the inflammation settle. | Niks wat u in hierdie fase met die spier doen, sal die senuwee vinniger laat herstel nie, en hard druk kan dinge later moeiliker maak. Die program is dus doelbewus rustig: beskerm die oog, hou die weefsel soepel, ondersteun die rusposisie van die gesig, en laat die ontsteking bedaar. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f1` | clinical | Eye protection and lubrication | Oogbeskerming en -bevogtiging | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f2` | clinical | Passive support, no forced movement | Passiewe ondersteuning, geen geforseerde beweging nie | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f3` | clinical | Gentle massage for circulation | Sagte masseer vir bloedsomloop | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.focus.f4` | clinical | Sleep and rest | Slaap en rus | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.short` | clinical | The nerve is inflamed. The work is protection, not effort. | Die senuwee is ontsteek. Die werk is beskerming, nie inspanning nie. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.tapeMode` | clinical | Resting support — lifts the mid-face against gravity. No pull into movement. | Rusondersteuning — lig die middelgesig teen swaartekrag. Geen trek na beweging nie. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.window` | clinical | Usually days 1–14 from onset | Gewoonlik dag 1–14 ná aanvang | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a2` | clinical | Long sets — fatigue makes the pattern worse | Lang stelle — moegheid maak die patroon erger | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a3` | clinical | Comparing the two sides mid-exercise | Die twee kante tydens die oefening vergelyk | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f2` | clinical | Low-effort, high-accuracy repetitions | Herhalings met min inspanning en groot akkuraatheid | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f3` | clinical | Mirror feedback for direction | Spieëlterugvoer vir rigting | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f4` | clinical | Keeping the unaffected side quiet | Die onaangetaste kant rustig hou | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.name` | clinical | Activate | Aktiveer | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.short` | clinical | The first signals are arriving. We help the brain find them. | Die eerste seine kom deur. Ons help die brein om hulle te vind. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.tapeMode` | clinical | Assist — tape helps the movement you are trying to make, so less effort is needed. | Help — die kleefband help met die beweging wat u probeer maak, sodat minder inspanning nodig is. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.window` | clinical | Usually weeks 2–6, once any flicker of movement appears | Gewoonlik weke 2–6, sodra enige flikkering van beweging verskyn | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a1` | clinical | Fast repetitions | Vinnige herhalings | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a2` | clinical | Practising the linked pattern you are trying to unlearn | Die gekoppelde patroon oefen wat u probeer afleer | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.avoid.a3` | clinical | Working past the point of fatigue | Verby die punt van moegheid werk | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.body` | clinical | At this stage most people can produce movement but not isolate it — the eye narrows when you smile, or the corner of the mouth pulls when you close the eye. Coordination work teaches separation. This is slower and less satisfying than strength work, and it matters more. | In hierdie fase kan die meeste mense beweging maak, maar dit nie isoleer nie — die oog vernou wanneer u glimlag, of die mondhoek trek wanneer u die oog toemaak. Koördinasiewerk leer skeiding. Dit is stadiger en minder bevredigend as kragwerk, en dit is belangriker. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f1` | clinical | Separating eye movement from mouth movement | Oogbeweging van mondbeweging skei | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f2` | clinical | Slow, controlled range through the middle of movement | Stadige, beheerde omvang deur die middel van die beweging | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f3` | clinical | Holding a position rather than snapping to it | 'n Posisie hou eerder as om daarna te ruk | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.focus.f4` | clinical | Relaxation between repetitions | Ontspanning tussen herhalings | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.name` | clinical | Coordination | Koördinasie | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.short` | clinical | Movement exists. Now it needs to go where you send it. | Daar is beweging. Nou moet dit gaan waarheen u dit stuur. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.tapeMode` | clinical | Guide — tape gives a directional cue and discourages the linked movement. | Lei — die kleefband gee 'n rigtingwyser en ontmoedig die gekoppelde beweging. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.3.window` | clinical | Usually weeks 6–16 | Gewoonlik weke 6–16 | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.avoid.a1` | clinical | Maximum-effort holds | Houe met maksimum inspanning | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.avoid.a2` | clinical | Ignoring tightness because movement is present | Styfheid ignoreer omdat daar beweging is | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.body` | clinical | Refinement is about the movements you use without thinking: speaking, eating, expressions in conversation. The exercises get closer to real life and further from the mirror, and tension management becomes as important as activation. | Verfyning gaan oor die bewegings wat u gebruik sonder om te dink: praat, eet, gesigsuitdrukkings in 'n gesprek. Die oefeninge kom nader aan die werklike lewe en verder van die spieël af, en die hantering van spanning word net so belangrik soos aktivering. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f1` | clinical | Expression during speech | Gesigsuitdrukking tydens praat | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f2` | clinical | Symmetry at conversational effort, not maximum effort | Simmetrie by gespreksinspanning, nie maksimum inspanning nie | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f4` | clinical | Endurance across the day | Uithouvermoë deur die dag | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.name` | clinical | Refine | Verfyn | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.short` | clinical | The face works. We are after control at low effort. | Die gesig werk. Ons is ná beheer met min inspanning. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.tapeMode` | clinical | Occasional — used for specific tasks or tiredness, not daily. | Af en toe — gebruik vir spesifieke take of moegheid, nie daagliks nie. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.window` | clinical | Usually months 4–9 | Gewoonlik maande 4–9 | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.avoid.a1` | clinical | Stopping entirely and losing range quietly | Heeltemal ophou en ongemerk omvang verloor | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.body` | clinical | Recovery flattens out but does not usually reverse. A shorter maintenance routine protects range and keeps tightness from creeping back, with periodic scans so any drift is caught early rather than discovered late. | Herstel plat af, maar keer gewoonlik nie om nie. 'n Korter onderhoudsroetine beskerm omvang en keer dat styfheid terugsluip, met periodieke skanderings sodat enige afwyking vroeg opgemerk word eerder as laat ontdek. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f1` | clinical | Short daily maintenance routine | Kort daaglikse onderhoudsroetine | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f2` | clinical | Stretching and release work | Strek- en loslaatwerk | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f3` | clinical | Periodic scans to confirm stability | Periodieke skanderings om stabiliteit te bevestig | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.focus.f4` | clinical | Returning to full activity | Terugkeer na volle aktiwiteit | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.name` | clinical | Maintain | Onderhou | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.short` | clinical | Holding what you have built, and using it. | Behou wat u opgebou het, en gebruik dit. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.tapeMode` | clinical | Not routine. Available for tiring days or specific events. | Nie roetine nie. Beskikbaar vir uitputtende dae of spesifieke geleenthede. | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.5.window` | clinical | From around month 9 | Vanaf ongeveer maand 9 | Stage description, timing, focus and things to avoid. |
| REVIEW | `programme.taping.directionalGuide` | clinical | Directional guide | Rigtinggeleiding | Taping mode name. |
| REVIEW | `programme.taping.movementAssist` | clinical | Movement assist | Bewegingshulp | Taping mode name. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.quote` | clinical | Stage-matching is the part that matters clinically. Handing every patient the same exercise sheet regardless of whether they have synkinesis is common practice and it is not good practice. This does better than that. | Om die fase te laat pas, is die deel wat klinies saak maak. Om elke pasiënt dieselfde oefenblad te gee, ongeag of hulle sinkinese het, is algemene praktyk, en dit is nie goeie praktyk nie. Dit doen beter as dit. | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.role` | clinical | Neurorehabilitation, university hospital | Neurorehabilitasie, universiteitshospitaal | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.aubert.where` | clinical | Lille | Lille | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.rahn.quote` | clinical | What is unusual here is the restraint. The programme does not promise recovery, does not grade patients against each other, and is explicit about where the evidence is thin. I refer patients to it and I read the progress reports they send me. | Wat hier ongewoon is, is die selfbeheersing. Die program belowe nie herstel nie, gradeer nie pasiënte teen mekaar nie, en is eksplisiet oor waar die bewyse dun is. Ek verwys pasiënte daarheen en ek lees die vorderingsverslae wat hulle vir my stuur. | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.rahn.where` | clinical | Vienna | Wene | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.solberg.quote` | clinical | I use the scan timeline in my own clinic sessions. Being able to show a patient objective change during a nine-week plateau does something for them that my reassurance alone does not. | Ek gebruik die skanderingstydlyn in my eie kliniese sessies. Om 'n pasiënt tydens 'n plato van nege weke objektiewe verandering te kan wys, doen iets vir hulle wat my gerusstelling alleen nie doen nie. | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.solberg.role` | clinical | Specialist facial physiotherapist | Gespesialiseerde gesigsfisioterapeut | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.solberg.where` | clinical | Oslo | Oslo | Clinician endorsement. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.body` | clinical | Marta woke on a Tuesday unable to close her right eye. Steroids started within 48 hours. She began Palsya at week two, in Stage 1, and spent eleven days doing almost nothing but eye protection and light massage — which she describes as the hardest part of the whole programme. The first flicker in her cheek came at day 19. Synkinesis appeared around week seven, which she found more distressing than the original paralysis. Coordination work through Stages 3 and 4 reduced it substantially but not completely. Five months in, she has mild eye narrowing when she smiles broadly and full function otherwise. | Marta het op 'n Dinsdag wakker geword en kon nie haar regteroog toemaak nie. Steroïede is binne 48 uur begin. Sy het in week twee met Palsya begin, in fase 1, en elf dae lank byna niks anders gedoen as oogbeskerming en ligte masseer nie — wat sy beskryf as die moeilikste deel van die hele program. Die eerste flikkering in haar wang het op dag 19 gekom. Sinkinese het omstreeks week sewe verskyn, wat sy meer ontstellend gevind het as die oorspronklike verlamming. Koördinasiewerk deur fases 3 en 4 het dit aansienlik verminder, maar nie heeltemal nie. Vyf maande later vernou haar oog effens wanneer sy breed glimlag, en andersins het sy volle funksie. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.dx` | clinical | Bell's palsy | Bell se verlamming | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.quote` | clinical | The stillness at the start felt like giving up. It was not. I understand that now and I did not then. | Die stilte aan die begin het gevoel soos opgee. Dit was nie. Ek verstaan dit nou, en toe het ek nie. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.summary` | clinical | From a complete right-sided palsy to eating and speaking normally, with mild synkinesis remaining. | Van 'n volledige regterkantse verlamming tot normale eet en praat, met ligte sinkinese wat oorbly. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.marta.where` | clinical | Warsaw | Warskou | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.body` | clinical | Sanne's paralysis followed surgery, which puts her in a different category from Bell's palsy: the recovery pattern was less predictable and the timeline longer. She used the six-consultation package throughout and stayed with the same clinician for nine months. Her mouth recovered substantially, her eye partially, and her forehead barely at all — an uneven pattern that she says was easier to accept once someone explained why it happens rather than treating it as a shortfall. She continues in Stage 5 with a short maintenance routine and a scan every six weeks. | Sanne se verlamming het op 'n operasie gevolg, wat haar in 'n ander kategorie as Bell se verlamming plaas: die herstelpatroon was minder voorspelbaar en die tydlyn langer. Sy het die hele tyd die pakket met ses konsultasies gebruik en nege maande lank by dieselfde klinikus gebly. Haar mond het aansienlik herstel, haar oog gedeeltelik, en haar voorkop skaars — 'n ongelyke patroon wat sy sê makliker was om te aanvaar sodra iemand verduidelik het waarom dit gebeur, eerder as om dit as 'n tekortkoming te behandel. Sy gaan voort in fase 5 met 'n kort onderhoudsroetine en 'n skandering elke ses weke. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.dx` | clinical | Post-surgical | Ná 'n operasie | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.quote` | clinical | I stopped measuring myself against the face I used to have. That was not a defeat. It was the thing that let me get on with it. | Ek het opgehou om myself te meet aan die gesig wat ek vroeër gehad het. Dit was nie 'n nederlaag nie. Dit was wat my in staat gestel het om aan te gaan. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.where` | clinical | Utrecht | Utrecht | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.body` | clinical | Tomás came to Palsya late, at week six, with no movement at all and having been told to wait and see. His first eight scans moved by less than four points, which is inside the measurement margin and therefore not really movement at all. He describes weeks seven through fifteen as the point where he stopped believing anything would change. His clinician kept him in Stage 2 through that entire period rather than advancing him, on the reasoning that advancing a programme ahead of the function is how compensations get established. Movement became clearly voluntary at week sixteen. By month seven his symmetry score had risen twenty-two points from baseline. | Tomás het laat na Palsya gekom, in week ses, sonder enige beweging en nadat hy aangesê is om maar te wag en te kyk. Sy eerste agt skanderings het minder as vier punte verskuif, wat binne die meetmarge is en dus eintlik glad nie beweging nie. Hy beskryf weke sewe tot vyftien as die punt waar hy opgehou het om te glo dat enigiets sou verander. Sy klinikus het hom die hele tydperk in fase 2 gehou eerder as om hom te laat vorder, met die redenasie dat kompensasies gevestig raak wanneer 'n program voor die funksie uit beweeg. Beweging het in week sestien duidelik willekeurig geword. Teen maand sewe het sy simmetrietelling twee-en-twintig punte bo die basislyn gestyg. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.dx` | clinical | Bell's palsy | Bell se verlamming | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.quote` | clinical | Nine weeks of flat lines. My physio would not move me on and she was right, but I resented her for about a month. | Nege weke van plat lyne. My fisio wou my nie laat aanbeweeg nie, en sy was reg, maar ek was omtrent 'n maand lank vies vir haar. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.summary` | clinical | A slow start, a nine-week plateau, and steady change after it. | 'n Stadige begin, 'n plato van nege weke, en bestendige verandering daarna. | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.tomas.where` | clinical | Porto | Porto | Patient case story with clinical detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.aisling.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Cork · started at week 2 | Bell se verlamming · Cork · begin in week 2 | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.aisling.quote` | clinical | I could see the change in the photo timeline before I could see it in the mirror. That mattered more than I expected during the weeks where nothing seemed to be happening. | Ek kon die verandering in die fototydlyn sien voordat ek dit in die spieël kon sien. Dit het meer saak gemaak as wat ek verwag het in die weke toe dit gelyk het asof niks gebeur nie. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.dieter.ctx` | clinical | Ramsay Hunt syndrome · Leipzig · started at week 6 | Ramsay Hunt-sindroom · Leipzig · begin in week 6 | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.dieter.quote` | clinical | Ramsay Hunt, so a slower road than most. What I appreciated was that nobody pretended otherwise or promised me a timeline they could not know. | Ramsay Hunt, so 'n stadiger pad as vir die meeste. Wat ek waardeer het, was dat niemand anders voorgegee of my 'n tydlyn belowe het wat hulle nie kon weet nie. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.lena.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Malmö · started at week 1 | Bell se verlamming · Malmö · begin in week 1 | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.lena.quote` | clinical | The eye care instructions were more useful than anything I got at the hospital, where I was handed a leaflet and sent home. My cornea is fine and I think that is why. | Die instruksies vir oogsorg was nuttiger as enigiets wat ek in die hospitaal gekry het, waar ek 'n pamflet gekry het en huis toe gestuur is. My kornea is in orde en ek dink dit is hoekom. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.marek.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Kraków · started at week 3 | Bell se verlamming · Krakau · begin in week 3 | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.marek.quote` | clinical | The bit that helped most was being told to do less. I had been forcing the smile as hard as I could, twice a day, for five weeks. My physio on the second call spotted it in about a minute. | Wat die meeste gehelp het, was om te hoor ek moet minder doen. Ek het die glimlag vyf weke lank twee keer per dag so hard as moontlik geforseer. My fisio het dit in die tweede oproep binne sowat 'n minuut raakgesien. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.rosa.ctx` | clinical | Bell's palsy · Valencia · started at week 4 | Bell se verlamming · Valencia · begin in week 4 | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.rosa.quote` | clinical | I am 71 and not good with apps. It is one screen with one button. My daughter set it up and I have used it on my own since the second day. | Ek is 71 en nie goed met toeps nie. Dit is een skerm met een knoppie. My dogter het dit opgestel en ek gebruik dit sedert die tweede dag op my eie. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.youssef.ctx` | clinical | Post-surgical facial nerve injury · Lyon | Gesigsenuweebesering ná 'n operasie · Lyon | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `stories.testimonials.youssef.quote` | clinical | Six months in, my face is not the face I had. But I can drink from a glass, I can eat in a restaurant, and I stopped avoiding photographs. That was the actual goal. | Ses maande later is my gesig nie die gesig wat ek gehad het nie. Maar ek kan uit 'n glas drink, ek kan in 'n restaurant eet, en ek het opgehou om foto's te vermy. Dit was die eintlike doel. | Testimonial with diagnosis/eye care detail. |
| REVIEW | `auth.signUp.footnote` | legal | No card required. You will not be charged before day 8. | Geen kaart nodig nie. U sal nie voor dag 8 gehef word nie. | Charge terms. |
| REVIEW | `clinician.patient.notes.hint` | legal | Notes are part of the patient's record and can't be edited or deleted. Up to {max} characters. | Notas vorm deel van die pasiënt se rekord en kan nie gewysig of uitgevee word nie. Hoogstens {max} karakters. | Notes are part of the patient record and cannot be edited/deleted — rendered as "pasiënt se rekord"; confirm medico-legal wording. |

| REVIEW | `common.footer.trust.gdpr` | legal | GDPR compliant | Voldoen aan GDPR |  |
| REVIEW | `emails.changed.notYou` | legal | If this wasn't you, contact support straight away at {support}. | As dit nie u was nie, kontak dadelik ondersteuning by {support}. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.cost.items.cancel.a` | legal | Yes, any time, from your account settings, in two clicks. No phone call, no retention offer. If you cancel during the free trial you are not charged at all. | Ja, enige tyd, vanuit u rekeninginstellings, in twee klikke. Geen oproep nie, geen aanbod om u te hou nie. As u tydens die gratis proeftydperk kanselleer, word u glad nie gehef nie. | Cancellation terms. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.cost.items.pricing.a` | legal | Three-month packages: {app} for the app alone, {guided} with two video consultations, {supported} with six. There is a {trialDays}-day free trial and you are not charged until day {chargeDay}. After three months you can extend month to month at {extension}, or stop. Extra consultations are {consult} each. | Pakkette van drie maande: {app} vir slegs die toep, {guided} met twee videokonsultasies, {supported} met ses. Daar is 'n gratis proeftydperk van {trialDays} dae en u word nie voor dag {chargeDay} gehef nie. Ná drie maande kan u maand vir maand verleng teen {extension}, of ophou. Ekstra konsultasies kos {consult} elk. | Pricing and trial terms. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.cost.items.whyThreeMonths.a` | legal | Because meaningful change in facial rehabilitation happens across months, and a one-month subscription would set you up to conclude it was not working right before it did. Three months is roughly the shortest honest commitment. | Omdat betekenisvolle verandering in gesigsrehabilitasie oor maande gebeur, en 'n intekening van een maand u sou laat aflei dat dit nie werk nie, net voordat dit wel begin werk. Drie maande is ongeveer die kortste eerlike verbintenis. |  |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.dataStored.a` | legal | On servers in the European Union, encrypted at rest and in transit. We are GDPR compliant. Facial images are treated as special category biometric data under Article 9 and handled accordingly. | Op bedieners in die Europese Unie, geënkripteer in rus en tydens oordrag. Ons voldoen aan die GDPR. Gesigsbeelde word ingevolge artikel 9 as biometriese data van 'n spesiale kategorie beskou en dienooreenkomstig hanteer. | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.dataStored.q` | legal | Where is my data stored? | Waar word my data gestoor? | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.exportDelete.a` | legal | Both, from your account settings. Export gives you your scans, sessions, and reports in a portable format. Deletion is permanent and removes your images within 30 days. | Albei, vanuit u rekeninginstellings. 'n Uitvoer gee u u skanderings, sessies en verslae in 'n draagbare formaat. Uitvee is permanent en verwyder u beelde binne 30 dae. | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.exportDelete.q` | legal | Can I get my data out, or delete it? | Kan ek my data uitkry, of dit uitvee? | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.sellData.a` | legal | No. Not to advertisers, not to insurers, not to researchers. Research use is opt-in, separate, aggregated, and refusing it changes nothing about your programme. | Nee. Nie aan adverteerders nie, nie aan versekeraars nie, nie aan navorsers nie. Gebruik vir navorsing is slegs met u toestemming, afsonderlik en saamgevoeg, en as u weier, verander dit niks aan u program nie. | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.items.sellData.q` | legal | Do you sell my data? | Verkoop julle my data? | Data storage/GDPR wording. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.privacy.title` | legal | Privacy | Privaatheid |  |

| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.clinicianShare.body` | legal | Only with someone you name, only what you send, and revocable. If you have a referral code from your own physiotherapist, this is the one that lets them see your reports. | Slegs met iemand wat u noem, slegs wat u stuur, en herroepbaar. As u 'n verwysingskode van u eie fisioterapeut het, is dit dié een wat hulle u verslae laat sien. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.clinicianShare.title` | legal | Sharing my progress with a clinician I choose | Deel van my vordering met 'n klinikus wat ek kies | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.dataProcessing.body` | legal | Your assessment answers, stage, session logs, and notes. This is special category data under GDPR Article 9 and we process it on your explicit consent. Without it there is no programme to run. | U assesseringsantwoorde, fase, sessielogboeke en notas. Dit is data van 'n spesiale kategorie ingevolge artikel 9 van die GDPR, en ons verwerk dit met u uitdruklike toestemming. Daarsonder is daar geen program om te bedryf nie. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.dataProcessing.title` | legal | Processing my health data | Verwerking van my gesondheidsdata | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.errRequired` | legal | The two required consents are needed for the programme to work. | Die twee verpligte toestemmings is nodig sodat die program kan werk. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.eyebrow` | legal | Consent and privacy | Toestemming en privaatheid | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.gdprNote` | legal | Your health data is special category data under GDPR and is processed only on your explicit consent, encrypted at rest and in transit, and never sold or used for advertising. | U gesondheidsdata is data van 'n spesiale kategorie ingevolge die GDPR en word slegs met u uitdruklike toestemming verwerk, geënkripteer in rus en tydens oordrag, en nooit verkoop of vir advertensies gebruik nie. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.heading` | legal | What you are agreeing to, in plain words | Waartoe u instem, in gewone woorde | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.laterNote` | legal | <b>You can change any of these later</b>, from Settings → Privacy and data. Withdrawing consent for photo storage deletes the stored images; your scores stay unless you delete those separately. | <b>U kan enigeen hiervan later verander</b>, by Instellings → Privaatheid en data. As u toestemming vir die stoor van foto's terugtrek, word die gestoorde beelde uitgevee; u tellings bly behoue tensy u dit afsonderlik uitvee. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.lead` | legal | Two of these are required because the programme cannot function without them. The rest genuinely change nothing about your programme or your price if you decline. | Twee hiervan is verpligtend omdat die program nie daarsonder kan werk nie. Die res verander werklik niks aan u program of u prys as u weier nie. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.marketing.body` | legal | Not a newsletter treadmill. Roughly monthly, and unsubscribing takes one click. | Nie 'n eindelose nuusbrief nie. Ongeveer maandeliks, en om te kanselleer, neem een klik. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.marketing.title` | legal | Occasional emails about new guides and features | Af en toe e-posse oor nuwe gidse en funksies | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.meta.description` | legal | What you are agreeing to, in plain words. | Waartoe u instem, in gewone woorde. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.meta.title` | legal | Consent and privacy — Palsya | Toestemming en privaatheid — Palsya | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.optional` | legal | Optional | Opsioneel | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.photoKeep.body` | legal | Kept encrypted, visible only to you and to clinicians you share with. You can delete any single photo, or all of them, at any time, and that deletion is real. | Geënkripteer bewaar, slegs sigbaar vir u en vir klinici met wie u dit deel. U kan enige enkele foto, of almal, op enige tyd uitvee, en daardie uitvee is werklik. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.photoKeep.title` | legal | Storing my scan photos so I can see my timeline | Stoor van my skanderingsfoto's sodat ek my tydlyn kan sien | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.photoStorage.body` | legal | Landmark detection runs on your device. <b>Storing</b> the images is the next item and is separate — decline that and you still get scores, just no photo timeline. | Landmerkbepaling gebeur op u toestel. Om die beelde te <b>stoor</b>, is die volgende item en is afsonderlik — as u dit weier, kry u steeds tellings, net geen fototydlyn nie. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.photoStorage.title` | legal | Analysing scans of my face | Ontleding van skanderings van my gesig | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.required` | legal | Required | Verpligtend | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.researchOptIn.body` | legal | Aggregated and stripped of identifiers, used to understand what helps. Never sold. Declining changes nothing about your programme, your price, or your clinician. | Saamgevoeg en van identifiseerders gestroop, gebruik om te verstaan wat help. Nooit verkoop nie. As u weier, verander dit niks aan u program, u prys of u klinikus nie. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.researchOptIn.title` | legal | Using my anonymised data for research | Gebruik van my geanonimiseerde data vir navorsing | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.submit` | legal | Agree and continue | Stem in en gaan voort | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.consent.termsAgreement` | legal | By continuing you agree to Palsya’s <link>Terms of Service and Privacy Policy</link>. | Deur voort te gaan, stem u in tot Palsya se <link>diensbepalings en privaatheidsbeleid</link>. | Consent wording (GDPR Art. 9, explicit consent). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.trialSummary` | legal | {price} for three months, charged on {date} unless you cancel before then. We will email you on day 5 to remind you, which is the opposite of how this usually works. | {price} vir drie maande, gehef op {date} tensy u voor dan kanselleer. Ons sal u op dag 5 'n e-pos stuur om u te herinner, wat die teenoorgestelde is van hoe dit gewoonlik werk. | Trial charge terms. |
| REVIEW | `passwordReset.forgot.sent` | legal | If an account exists for this email, we've sent a reset link. | As daar 'n rekening vir hierdie e-posadres bestaan, het ons 'n herstelskakel gestuur. | Security wording kept non-committal: "As daar 'n rekening vir hierdie e-posadres bestaan, het ons 'n herstelskakel gestuur." |
| REVIEW | `pricing.extras.referral.body` | legal | {discount, number, percent} off your first three months. Enter their referral code at checkout — it also lets them see your progress reports, if you agree to that separately. | {discount, number, percent} afslag op u eerste drie maande. Voer hul verwysingskode by die betaalpunt in — dit laat hulle ook u vorderingsverslae sien, as u afsonderlik daartoe instem. | Referral data-sharing consent. |
| REVIEW | `about.story.p2` | terminology | He did the exercises. Hard, twice a day, at maximum effort, for eleven weeks — which is close to the worst thing he could have done, and nobody told him otherwise until he found a specialist physiotherapist privately in month four. By then the synkinesis was established and it took another six months of coordination work to reduce it. | Hy het die oefeninge gedoen. Hard, twee keer per dag, met maksimum inspanning, elf weke lank — wat naastenby die ergste ding is wat hy kon doen, en niemand het hom anders vertel nie totdat hy in die vierde maand privaat 'n gespesialiseerde fisioterapeut gevind het. Teen daardie tyd was die sinkinese gevestig, en dit het nog ses maande se koördinasiewerk geverg om dit te verminder. | 'synkinesis' = 'sinkinese' throughout; confirm SA clinical form (vs 'sinkinesie'). |
| REVIEW | `admin.audit.actions.user_demoted_self_registered` | terminology | Self-registered clinician demoted | Selfgeregistreerde klinikus afgegradeer | "demoted" = "afgegradeer" — please confirm. |
| REVIEW | `admin.roles.admin` | terminology | Admin | Administrateur | Role Admin = "Administrateur" (plural "Administrateurs"); admin area itself stays "Administrasie" as in admin.json. Patient = Pasiënt, Clinician = Klinikus/Klinici. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.bilateral.body` | terminology | Facial weakness on both sides is uncommon and is more often linked to a systemic cause — Lyme disease, Guillain-Barré syndrome, sarcoidosis, among others. This should be investigated before you begin a rehabilitation programme. | Swakheid aan albei kante van die gesig is ongewoon en hou meer dikwels verband met 'n sistemiese oorsaak — onder andere Lyme-siekte, Guillain-Barré-sindroom of sarkoïdose. Dit moet ondersoek word voordat u met 'n rehabilitasieprogram begin. | 'Guillain-Barre-sindroom', 'sarkoidose', 'sistemiese oorsaak': confirm spelling. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.estim.body` | terminology | We do not include electrical stimulation. The evidence is inconsistent and there is a theoretical concern that stimulation during reinnervation may worsen synkinesis. If a clinician prescribed it for you specifically, follow their advice — but please raise it on your first consultation. | Ons sluit nie elektriese stimulasie in nie. Die bewyse is teenstrydig en daar is 'n teoretiese kommer dat stimulasie tydens herinnervasie sinkinese kan vererger. As 'n klinikus dit spesifiek vir u voorgeskryf het, volg hul advies — maar bring dit asseblief tydens u eerste konsultasie ter sprake. | 'reinnervation' = 'herinnervasie'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.eye.body` | terminology | Eye pain, persistent redness, or changed vision with facial paralysis can mean the cornea is being damaged by exposure. This is the one complication that can cause lasting harm and it is treatable. Please get it looked at today — an optometrist, GP, or eye casualty department. | Oogpyn, aanhoudende rooiheid of veranderde sig saam met gesigsverlamming kan beteken dat die kornea deur blootstelling beskadig word. Dit is die een komplikasie wat blywende skade kan veroorsaak, en dit is behandelbaar. Laat dit asseblief vandag ondersoek — deur 'n optometris, 'n huisdokter of 'n oognoodafdeling. | 'GP' = 'huisdokter'; 'eye casualty department' = 'oognoodafdeling' (no fixed SA equivalent). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.limb.body` | terminology | Facial weakness together with weakness or numbness in an arm or leg can be a stroke. This needs emergency assessment, not a rehabilitation programme. Please stop here and call your local emergency number. | Swakheid in die gesig saam met swakheid of gevoelloosheid in 'n arm of been kan 'n beroerte wees. Dit het 'n noodondersoek nodig, nie 'n rehabilitasieprogram nie. Hou asseblief hier op en bel u plaaslike noodnommer. | 'call your local emergency number' kept generic ('u plaaslike noodnommer'). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.noEyeCare.body` | terminology | Your eye does not close fully and you are not using anything to protect it. Preservative-free lubricating drops are available without prescription and should start today, with ointment at night. This matters more than any exercise in your programme. | U oog maak nie heeltemal toe nie en u gebruik niks om dit te beskerm nie. Bevogtigende oogdruppels sonder preserveermiddels is sonder voorskrif beskikbaar en moet vandag begin word, met oogsalf in die nag. Dit is belangriker as enige oefening in u program. | 'Preservative-free lubricating drops' = 'bevogtigende oogdruppels sonder preserveermiddels'; 'ointment' = 'oogsalf'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.flags.tooEarly.body` | terminology | You are {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} from onset. Corticosteroids started within the first 72 hours measurably improve outcomes in Bell's palsy, and that window matters more right now than starting exercises. Nothing you do with the muscle in the first week changes the nerve.  Come back from day 5. We will hold your answers. | Dit is {days, plural, one {# dag} other {# dae}} sedert aanvang. Kortikosteroïede wat binne die eerste 72 uur begin word, verbeter die uitkomste by Bell se verlamming meetbaar, en daardie tydsvenster is nou belangriker as om met oefeninge te begin. Niks wat u in die eerste week met die spier doen, verander die senuwee nie.  Kom terug vanaf dag 5. Ons sal u antwoorde bewaar. | 'Corticosteroids' = 'kortikosteroiede'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.bells.d` | terminology | Idiopathic facial palsy — no specific cause identified. | Idiopatiese gesigsverlamming — geen spesifieke oorsaak geïdentifiseer nie. | 'Idiopathic facial palsy' = 'idiopatiese gesigsverlamming'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.lyme.t` | terminology | Lyme disease | Lyme-siekte | 'Lyme-siekte': confirm form. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.diagnosis.options.ramsay.d` | terminology | Caused by the varicella zoster virus, usually with a rash. | Veroorsaak deur die varicella-zoster-virus, gewoonlik met 'n uitslag. | 'varicella-zoster-virus' spelling; 'rash' = 'uitslag'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeCare.options.chamber.t` | terminology | A moisture chamber or eye shield | 'n Vogkamer of oogskerm | 'moisture chamber' = 'vogkamer'; 'eye shield' = 'oogskerm'. Unsure of established SA term. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.dryness.t` | terminology | Dryness or grittiness | Droogheid of 'n sanderige gevoel | 'grittiness' = 'sanderige gevoel'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.eyeSymptoms.options.watering.t` | terminology | Excessive watering | Oormatige traanvloei | 'Excessive watering' = 'oormatige traanvloei'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.otherSymptoms.options.tinnitus.t` | terminology | Ringing in the ear | Gesuis in die oor | 'Ringing in the ear' = 'gesuis in die oor' (lay term). |
| REVIEW | `assessment.questions.tightness.q` | terminology | Does the affected side of your face feel tight or stiff? | Voel die aangetaste kant van u gesig styf of stram? | 'tight or stiff' = 'styf of stram'. |
| REVIEW | `assessment.reasons.noMovement` | terminology | No voluntary movement in any region, so the programme starts with protection rather than activation. | Geen willekeurige beweging in enige area nie, dus begin die program met beskerming eerder as aktivering. | 'willekeurige beweging' for voluntary movement: same concern as programme.stages.2.focus.f1. |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.score.title` | terminology | Symmetry score | Simmetrietelling | 'symmetry score' = 'simmetrietelling' throughout. |
| REVIEW | `dashboard.session.taping.title` | terminology | Taping | Kleefband | 'taping' rendered as 'kleefband' / 'kleefband aanbring'. SA physios may say 'taping' or 'kinesiologiese kleefband'. Please confirm preferred term. |
| REVIEW | `guides.faq.scans.items.scoreMeaning.a` | terminology | It measures geometric symmetry, and nothing more. It is not a clinical severity grade, it does not translate to a House-Brackmann number, and it is not a percentage of recovery. Its only real job is comparing you today with you last month. Measurement variability is about ±2.5 points, so we treat smaller changes as noise. | Dit meet geometriese simmetrie, en niks meer nie. Dit is nie 'n kliniese graad van erns nie, dit kan nie na 'n House-Brackmann-getal omgeskakel word nie, en dit is nie 'n persentasie van herstel nie. Sy enigste werklike taak is om u vandag met u verlede maand te vergelyk. Die meetvariasie is sowat ±2,5 punte, so ons beskou kleiner veranderinge as ruis. | 'Measurement variability' = 'meetvariasie'; decimal comma in +-2,5. |
| REVIEW | `howItWorks.steps.scan.p4` | terminology | The score is a geometric measurement. It is not a severity grade, it does not convert to House-Brackmann, and it is not a percentage of recovery. Its whole purpose is comparing you today with you last month. | Die telling is 'n geometriese meting. Dit is nie 'n graad van erns nie, dit kan nie na House-Brackmann omgeskakel word nie, en dit is nie 'n persentasie van herstel nie. Die hele doel daarvan is om u vandag met u verlede maand te vergelyk. | 'severity grade' = 'graad van erns'; House-Brackmann kept. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.cheek.watch` | terminology | Do not do this one if you have had recent ear surgery or your clinician has told you to avoid pressure changes. | Moenie hierdie een doen as u onlangs 'n oorooperasie gehad het of u klinikus vir u gesê het om drukveranderinge te vermy nie. | 'ear surgery' = 'oorooperasie'. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.name` | terminology | Nostril flare | Neusvleuels sprei | 'Nostril flare' = 'Neusvleuels sprei' (coined). |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.nose.why` | terminology | The mid-face lifts the nasolabial fold and supports the upper lip. It responds well to small isolated work and is easy to skip. | Die middelgesig lig die nasolabiale plooi en ondersteun die bolip. Dit reageer goed op klein, geïsoleerde werk en word maklik oorgeslaan. | 'nasolabial fold' = 'nasolabiale plooi'; 'mid-face' = 'middelgesig'. |
| REVIEW | `library.exercises.release.watch` | terminology | Stop if you feel sharp pain, pins and needles, or increased twitching afterwards. | Hou op as u skerp pyn of tinteling voel, of as die spiertrekkings daarna toeneem. | 'pins and needles' = 'tinteling'; 'twitching' = 'spiertrekkings'. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.body` | terminology | Plosive and labial sounds need lip seal, so they double as an exercise you can do without a mirror. Read aloud slowly and exaggerate slightly. | Plofklanke en lipklanke het 'n lipseël nodig, so dit dien ook as 'n oefening wat u sonder 'n spieël kan doen. Lees stadig hardop en oordryf effens. | 'Plosive and labial sounds' = 'plofklanke en lipklanke'. |
| REVIEW | `library.routine.speech.items.i2` | terminology | Emphasise p, b, m, f, v, w | Beklemtoon p, b, m, f, v, w | Letter list p, b, m, f, v, w kept as in English; in Afrikaans 'v' is /f/ and 'w' is /v/, so the list may need adapting. |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.assist.strips.s1.where` | terminology | Zygomatic line | Zigomatiese lyn | 'Zygomatic line' = 'Zigomatiese lyn': may be too technical for patients. |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s1.note` | terminology | Never across the lid itself. Below the orbital rim only. | Nooit oor die ooglid self nie. Slegs onder die oogkasrand. | 'orbital rim' = 'oogkasrand'. |
| REVIEW | `library.taping.guide.strips.s2.where` | terminology | Cheek counter-cue | Teenwenk op die wang | 'Cheek counter-cue' = 'Teenwenk op die wang': coined phrase, check clarity. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.phase.confidenceLabel` | terminology | Confidence | Betroubaarheid | Placement 'Confidence' = 'Betroubaarheid'. |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.phase.typicalWindow` | terminology | Typical window | Tipiese tydperk | Time 'window' = 'tydperk' (elsewhere 'tydsvenster'). |
| REVIEW | `onboarding.ready.tapingHint` | terminology | You will need elastic therapeutic tape. Any brand. Patch-test it on your forearm first. | U sal elastiese terapeutiese kleefband nodig hê. Enige handelsmerk. Toets dit eers op u voorarm vir 'n velreaksie. | 'elastic therapeutic tape' = 'elastiese terapeutiese kleefband'; 'patch-test' rendered as testing on the forearm for a skin reaction. |
| REVIEW | `pricing.card.subscribe` | terminology | Subscribe | Teken in | 'Subscribe' = 'Teken in'; sign-in is consistently 'Meld aan'. Consider 'Word intekenaar' if confusing. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.1.name` | terminology | Protect | Beskerm | Stage names chosen: Beskerm / Aktiveer / Koordinasie / Verfyn / Onderhou (imperative verbs, as in English). Used everywhere; UI says 'fase' for both stage and phase. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.avoid.a1` | terminology | Effortful straining, which recruits the wrong muscles | Inspannende forsering, wat die verkeerde spiere inspan | 'recruits the wrong muscles' = 'die verkeerde spiere inspan'. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.body` | terminology | A flicker is enough to work with. The goal now is to find the smallest movement you can actually produce and repeat it accurately, rather than to produce a big movement badly. Small and correct beats large and recruited. | 'n Flikkering is genoeg om mee te werk. Die doel is nou om die kleinste beweging te vind wat u werklik kan maak en dit akkuraat te herhaal, eerder as om 'n groot beweging swak te maak. Klein en korrek is beter as groot met die verkeerde spiere. | 'Small and correct beats large and recruited' paraphrased as 'groot met die verkeerde spiere'. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.2.focus.f1` | terminology | Finding minimum voluntary movement | Die kleinste willekeurige beweging vind | 'voluntary movement' = 'willekeurige beweging' (standard physiology term, but lay readers may read 'willekeurig' as 'random'). Consider 'doelbewuste beweging'. |
| REVIEW | `programme.stages.4.focus.f3` | terminology | Releasing tightness and over-recruitment | Styfheid en oormatige spieraktivering losmaak | 'over-recruitment' = 'oormatige spieraktivering' (paraphrase). |
| REVIEW | `programme.taping.restingSupport` | terminology | Resting support | Rusondersteuning | Tape mode labels: Rusondersteuning / Bewegingshulp / Rigtinggeleiding. |
| REVIEW | `stories.endorsements.rahn.role` | terminology | Consultant Otolaryngologist | Konsultant-otolaringoloog | 'Consultant Otolaryngologist' = 'Konsultant-otolaringoloog' (SA lay usage: 'oor-, neus- en keelspesialis'). |
| REVIEW | `stories.items.sanne.summary` | terminology | Facial nerve injury after acoustic neuroma surgery, with a partial and asymmetric recovery. | Gesigsenuweebesering ná 'n operasie vir 'n akoestiese neuroom, met gedeeltelike en asimmetriese herstel. | 'acoustic neuroma' = 'akoestiese neuroom'. |
| REVIEW | `stories.press.derStandard.headline` | terminology | Wenn das Gesicht stillsteht: digitale Hilfe nach Fazialisparese | Wenn das Gesicht stillsteht: digitale Hilfe nach Fazialisparese | German headline kept in original German (published title), so identical to source. |
| REVIEW | `stories.press.jdr.headline` | terminology | Remote facial neuromuscular retraining: an observational cohort of 1,204 users | Neuromuskulêre heropleiding van die gesig op afstand: 'n waarnemingskohort van 1 204 gebruikers | Published English article title translated per brief rule 6; consider keeping the original since it is a citation. |
| REVIEW | `emails.invite.body` | tone | {inviter} has invited you to Palsya {role, select, admin {as an administrator} other {as a clinician}}. Accept the invitation to set your name and password. | {inviter} het u na Palsya uitgenooi {role, select, admin {as administrateur} other {as klinikus}}. Aanvaar die uitnodiging om u naam en wagwoord in te stel. | ICU select placed after "na Palsya uitgenooi" per English order; reads naturally in both branches. |
